Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET
Hangi Tablo için : OSF312
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Operasyon Etiketi
Kod : 
Public Ust, sol, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, d7LB, EtiketEni, EtiketSayisi
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	if trim(SFDC31E.GK_1) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "'Makara Bitti mi?' alaný doldurulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(SFDC31E.GK_1) <> "E" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bitmemiþ makaralar için etiket alýnamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	If UretimFisiOlusturulmamis() Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Etiket almadan önce üretim fiþi oluþturmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(SFDC31E.D7_ISLEM_KODU) <> "U" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca Üretimler için etiket alýnabilir", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub		
	End if
	if trim(GetUretimFisNo()) = "" Then
		if Doc.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	End if
	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 75
	sayfaEni = 100
	Ustmargin = 5
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 1
	EtiketBoyu = 75
	EtiketEni = 100
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 0
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 0
	EndenEtiketSayisi = 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1

	sayfaEtiketSayisi = EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi

	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	if EtiketSayisi is Nothing Then
'		EtiketSayisi = 1
'	Else
		if Doc.CDbl(EtiketSayisi) = 0 Then EtiketSayisi = 1
'	End if
	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		KutuMiktari = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			xEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo - 1) mod EndenEtiketSayisi
			yEtiketSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div((EtiketNo-1), EndenEtiketSayisi)) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi
			if EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 then
				Sol = SolMargin
			else
				Sol = SolMargin + xEtiketSayisi * (EtiketEni + EndenEtiketlerArasi)
			end if	
			if (EtiketNo mod sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1 or sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1) and EtiketNo > 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Ust = Ustmargin					
			else
				Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * (EtiketBoyu + BoydanEtiketlerArasi)
			end if
	
			Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
		Next
	Next
	if SFDC31E.ISPREV Then
		res = pe1.Show()
	Else
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
	sag = solMargin + EtiketEni - 10

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust, sag - 25, -2, 20, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.NoPen()

	Gen1 = 26
	Aralik = 0

'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Tarih")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Tarih")
'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, "")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Stok Kodu")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Stok Kodu")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & D7LB.SKOD(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",18)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom, sol + 1, -2, sag - sol, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD(satirNo), "")))

	OpKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(d7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)), 6)
	OpAdi = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", OpKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Operasyon Adý")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",18)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Trim(OpAdi))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",18)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(D7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo), 18, 6)) & " " & D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Ýþ Emri/Operasyon No")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(GetIsNoStr(D7LB.JOBNO(satirNo))))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2,38, ": " & trim(GetIsNoStr(D7LB.JOBNO(satirNo))))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))


	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 5, Gen1, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)))

	OperatorKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(d7LB.REFTEXT502(satirNo)), 16)
	OperatorAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", OperatorKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Operatör")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(OperatorAdi))

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	Barkod = D7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 6, sol + 10, 13, sag - sol - 20, "CODE128", trim(Barkod), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
End Sub
Function GetIsNoStr(IsNo)
	GetIsNoStr = Left(IsNo, 10) & " / " & Doc.CDbl(Right(IsNo, 6))
End Function
Function UretimFisiOlusturulmamis()
	UretimFisiOlusturulmamis = False
	if Left(SFDC31E.STOK_CODE, 1) = "3" Then Exit Function
	if trim(GetUretimFisNo()) = "" Then UretimFisiOlusturulmamis = True
End Function
Function GetUretimFisNo()
	GetUretimFisNo = ""
	SrNum1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", SFDC31E.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("adf3fafgda", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum1 & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set S20T = Doc.Select1("aef3fsds4sh", "STOK20T", "W_APP='OSF312'&W_TABLE='SFDC31E'&W_EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S20T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetUretimFisNo = S20T.EVRAKNO
		End if
	End if
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-BOL1
Hangi Tablo için : TRACK251
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK25, TRACK25
Açýklama : Etiket Bölme Etiketi
Kod : 
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Set S25T = Doc.Select1("de34frt5454", "STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='" & STACK.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S25T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Else
		Set S25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	End if
	SeriNo = S25T.NEWSERINO(1)
	if trim(SeriNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SeriNo), 30)
	EtiketFormKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "FORMKODU", Barkod, "")
	if trim(EtiketFormKodu) = "" Then
		if Left(S25T.KOD(1), 1) = "3" Then EtiketFormKodu ="ETK-MAMUL"
	End if
	if trim(EtiketFormKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu,"PrintLabel"
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-HMD
Hangi Tablo için : STOK29

Kod : 
Public Ust, sol, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, d7LB, EtiketEni, EtiketSayisi
Sub PrintLabel1(par1)
	EtiketSayisi = par1 
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" Then EtiketSayisi = 2

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 100
	Ustmargin = 7.5
	solMargin = 5
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 5
	EtiketBoyu = 100
	EtiketEni = 100
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 0
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 0
	EndenEtiketSayisi = 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 1

	sayfaEtiketSayisi = EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi

	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	if EtiketSayisi is Nothing Then
'		EtiketSayisi = 1
'	Else
		if Doc.CDbl(EtiketSayisi) = 0 Then EtiketSayisi = 1
'	End if
	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		KutuMiktari = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			xEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo - 1) mod EndenEtiketSayisi
			yEtiketSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div((EtiketNo-1), EndenEtiketSayisi)) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi
			if EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 then
				Sol = SolMargin
			else
				Sol = SolMargin + xEtiketSayisi * (EtiketEni + EndenEtiketlerArasi)
			end if	
			if (EtiketNo mod sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1 or sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1) and EtiketNo > 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Ust = Ustmargin					
			else
				Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * (EtiketBoyu + BoydanEtiketlerArasi)
			end if
	
			Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
		Next
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK251" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACKIZ" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
	sag = solMargin + EtiketEni - 10

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust, Sol + 30, 12, 40, logo, 114)
	res = pe1.NoPen()

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Black",16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, sol, 8, sag - sol, "MALZEME TANITIM KARTI")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 8, sol, 0, sag - sol)

	Gen1 = 24
	Aralik = 1
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Malzeme Kodu")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & D7LB.SKOD(satirNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Malzeme Adý")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD(satirNo), "")))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Lot No")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Tedarikçi")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", D7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(satirNo), "")))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + Aralik, sol + 1, 4, Gen1, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sag - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(D7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo), 15, 3) & " " & D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo))

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	Barkod = D7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + 8, sol + 10, 15, sag - sol - 20, "CODE128", trim(Barkod), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-MAMUL

Public Ust, sol, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, d7LB, EtiketEni, EtiketSayisi, CallerApp
Sub PrintLabel2(par1, par2)
	EtiketSayisi = par1
	CallerApp = par2
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel1(par1)
	EtiketSayisi = par1 
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	if CallerApp = "STOK20_Barkod" Then
		Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTableT.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)
	Else
		Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)
	End if
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK21" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" or Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then EtiketSayisi = 2

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 80
	sayfaEni = 60
	Ustmargin = 2
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 2
	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 60
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 0
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 0
	EndenEtiketSayisi = 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	sayfaEtiketSayisi = EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi

	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket", sayfaEni, sayfaBoyu, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	if EtiketSayisi is Nothing Then
'		EtiketSayisi = 1
'	Else
		if Doc.CDbl(EtiketSayisi) = 0 Then EtiketSayisi = 1
'	End if
	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		KutuMiktari = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			xEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo - 1) mod EndenEtiketSayisi
			yEtiketSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div((EtiketNo-1), EndenEtiketSayisi)) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi
			if EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 then
				Sol = SolMargin
			else
				Sol = SolMargin + xEtiketSayisi * (EtiketEni + EndenEtiketlerArasi)
			end if	
			if (EtiketNo mod sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1 or sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1) and EtiketNo > 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Ust = Ustmargin					
			else
				Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * (EtiketBoyu + BoydanEtiketlerArasi)
			end if
	
			Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
		Next
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK251" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACKIZ" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
'		res = pe1.Show()
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
	sag = solMargin + EtiketEni - 2

'	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
'	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
'	res = pe1.NoPen()

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, sol, 11, sag - sol)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, sag - sol - 2, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD(satirNo), "")))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 13, sol + 1, 4, 30, D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4, sol + 1, 4, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 15, sag - 32, 7, 30, trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(D7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo),18,3)) & " " & trim(D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	Barkod = D7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 22, sol + 5, 15, sag - sol - 10, "CODE128", trim(Barkod), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-MAMUL1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : D7KIDSLB
Açýklama : Mamul Barkod Etiketi
Kod : 
Public Ust, sol, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, d7LB, EtiketEni, EtiketSayisi
Sub PrintLabel1(par1)
	EtiketSayisi = par1 
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	EtiketFormu = SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET", "GetEtiketTable", EtiketFormu)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" Then EtiketSayisi = 2

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 60
	sayfaEni = 80
	Ustmargin = 2
	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 2
	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 60
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 0
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 0
	EndenEtiketSayisi = 1
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 2

	sayfaEtiketSayisi = EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi

	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		KutuMiktari = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(i)	
		For j = 1 to EtiketSayisi
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			xEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo - 1) mod EndenEtiketSayisi
			yEtiketSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div((EtiketNo-1), EndenEtiketSayisi)) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi
			if EtiketNo Mod EndenEtiketSayisi = 1 then
				Sol = SolMargin
			else
				Sol = SolMargin + xEtiketSayisi * (EtiketEni + EndenEtiketlerArasi)
			end if	
			if (EtiketNo mod sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1 or sayfaEtiketSayisi = 1) and EtiketNo > 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Ust = Ustmargin					
			else
				Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * (EtiketBoyu + BoydanEtiketlerArasi)
			end if
	
			Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
		Next
	Next
	if Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK251" or Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACKIZ" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
	sag = solMargin + EtiketEni - 2
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(sol, Ust, sag - sol, 11)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sol + 1, Ust + 0.5, sag - sol - 2, 12, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD(satirNo), "")))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	res = pe1.Write(sag - 31, Ust + 13, 30, 4, D7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.Write(sag - 31, Ust + 17, 30, 4, Doc.StrToNorDate(D7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sag - 50, Ust + 15, 30, 7, trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(D7LB.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo),18,3)) & " " & trim(D7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	Barkod = D7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(sol + 5, Ust + 22, 14, sag - sol - 10, "CODE128", trim(Barkod), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Sub PrintForm()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	
	satirBasla = 4
	
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	logo = trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm) & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	if not fso.FileExists(logo) Then logo = ""
	
	MMPS10T_LIST.Sort("REVURETIMDENTESTARIH")
	
	Sh1.Cells(1, 1).Select
	if trim(logo) <> "" Then
		Sh1.Pictures.Insert(logo).Select
	End if
	
	Sutun = 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "MPS No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Sipariþ No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Müþteri Sipariþ No"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Firma"
	' Sutun = Sutun + 1
	' Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Stok Kodu"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Stok Adý"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Ýþ Emri Miktarý"
	IEMikCol = Sutun
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Termin"
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Kalan Gün"
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T_LIST")
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		if Left(Grid(i,0), 5) = "COLVT" and Grid(i,2) <> "..." Then
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = trim(Grid(i,2))
		End if
	Next
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Sevkiyat Miktarý"
	SevkCol = Sutun
	Sutun = Sutun + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun).Value = "Rezerve Miktar"
	RezCol = Sutun
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, 1), Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, Sutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	
	satirNo = satirBasla
	QStr = "SELECT S01.KOD, STOK40T.EVRAKNO SIPNO, STOK40T.CHSIPNO CHSIPNO, MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO MPSNO, STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU,"
	QStr = QStr & " STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR SIPMIKTAR, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH,(STOK40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - STOK40T.SF_SEVKDEMIK) SEVKMIK,"
	QStr = QStr & " S01.SF_MIKTAR REZ_MIKTAR, MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR ISEMRIMIK"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM (SELECT KOD, ALLOC_SIPARTNO, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR "
	QStr = QStr & "  FROM STOK01 "
	QStr = QStr & "  WHERE STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO<>'                '"
	QStr = QStr & "  GROUP BY KOD, ALLOC_SIPARTNO) S01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='SP' AND STOK40T.EVRAKNO=MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS AND STOK40T.TRNUM=MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10TM.ACIK_KAPALI='K'"
	
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XSDSS", QStr
	Set S01X = Doc.GetTableObject("XSDSS")
	For i = 1 to S01X.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kapalý MPS 'lere Ait Bilgiler Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S01X.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = S01X.MPSNO(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Select
		Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 36
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S01X.SIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S01X.CHSIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S01X.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
		'  Sutun = Sutun + 1
		'  Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01X.KOD(i), ""))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = S01X.ISEMRIMIK(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = S01X.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(TeslimTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)
		
		SevkMik = S01X.SEVKMIK(i) / 1000
		if SevkMik > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, SevkCol).Value = SevkMik
		RezerveMiktar = S01X.REZ_MIKTAR(i) / 1000
		if RezerveMiktar > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, RezCol).Value = RezerveMiktar
	Next
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MMPS10T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "ACIK_KAPALI", MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i), "")) <> "K" Then
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			Sutun = 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Value = MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i)
			SipArr = GetSipArr(MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i))
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(0)
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(1)
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & SipArr(2)
			'  Sutun = Sutun + 1
			'  Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU_STOK00_AD(i))
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			IsEmriMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MMPS10T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i), "")
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			TeslimTarihi = MMPS10T_LIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
			TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(MMPS10T_LIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)
			Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "MMPS10T_LIST")
			DegerColBasla = Sutun + 1
			for j = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
				if Left(Grid(j,0), 5) = "COLVT" and Grid(j,2) <> "..." Then
					Sutun = Sutun + 1
					Deger = MMPS10T_LIST.GetFieldValue(Grid(j,0), i)
					DegerArr = FormatDeger(Deger)
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = DegerArr(0)
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Select
					if DegerArr(1) <> 0 Then
						Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = DegerArr(1)
					End if
				End if
			Next
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			if SipArr(4) > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = SipArr(4) / 1000
			Sutun = Sutun + 1
			if SipArr(3) > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = SipArr(3) / 1000
		End if
	Next
	sagSutun = Sutun
	
	QStr = "SELECT STOK40T.EVRAKNO, STOK40T.KOD, STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH, STOK40T.CHSIPNO, STOK40T.ARTNO FROM STOK40T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON STOK40T.ARTNO=MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL ORDER BY REV_RTESTARIH"
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "weg4tgt5", QStr
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("weg4tgt5")
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'e Baðlý Olmayan Sipariþler Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S40T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = ""
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S40T.EVRAKNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S40T.CHSIPNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
		'  Sutun = Sutun + 1
		'  Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(MMPS10T_LIST.R_MAMULKODU(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S40T.KOD(i), ""))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(TeslimTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)
		
		RezerveMiktar = GetSipeRezerveMik(S40T.ARTNO(i))
		if RezerveMiktar > 0 Then Sh1.Cells(satirNo, RezCol).Value = RezerveMiktar
	Next
	
	QStr = "SELECT STOK51T.EVRAKNO, STOK51T.TRNUM, STOK51T.KOD, STOK51T.SF_MIKTAR, STOK51T.RIMALATISTEKTARIH FROM STOK51T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='PC' AND MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS=STOK51T.EVRAKNO AND MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM=STOK51T.TRNUM"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK51T.ACIKKAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL ORDER BY RIMALATISTEKTARIH"
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "weg4tgt5", QStr
	Set S51T = Doc.GetTableObject("weg4tgt5")
	For i = 1 to S51T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'e baðlý olmayan Üretim Emirleri Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S51T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sutun = 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = ""
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'S-" & trim(S51T.EVRAKNO(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = ""
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = ""
		'  Sutun = Sutun + 1
		'  Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "'" & trim(S51T.KOD(i))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S51T.KOD(i), ""))
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		IsEmriMiktari = S51T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = IsEmriMiktari / 1000
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		TeslimTarihi = S51T.RIMALATISTEKTARIH(i)
		TeslimTarihi = DateSerial(Doc.StrDate_Year(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Month(TeslimTarihi), Doc.StrDate_Day(TeslimTarihi))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = TeslimTarihi
		Sutun = Sutun + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Doc.CDateToInt(TeslimTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.BuGun)
	Next
	
	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, IEMikCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)
	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, SevkCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)
	Call NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, RezCol, satirBasla + 1, satirNo)
	
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Name = "Arial Narrow"
	
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla+1, DegerColBasla), Sh1.Cells(satirNo, sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4152
	
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,sagSutun)).Select
	Sh1.Rows(satirBasla).RowHeight = 26.25
	Excel.Selection.VerticalAlignment = -4160
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit
	Excel.Columns(4).ColumnWidth = 11.71
	
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.PrintTitleRows = "$1:$" & satirBasla
	
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.PrintArea = Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Address
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.LeftMargin = 28.3 * 0.8
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.RightMargin = 28.3 * 0.8
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.TopMargin = 28.3 * 1
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.BottomMargin = 28.3 * 1
	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.FooterMargin = 28.3 * 0.8

	Excel.ActiveSheet.PageSetup.CenterFooter = "&P/&N"
	
	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = "ÜRETÝM GAZETESÝ"
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(2,1),Sh1.Cells(2,sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Size = 18
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = -4108
	Excel.Selection.Merge
	
	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 3).Value = "Tarih"
	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 3).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Sh1.Cells(1, sagSutun - 2).Value = ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun) & " " & Doc.Saat(5)
	
	
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(satirBasla,1),Sh1.Cells(satirNo,sagSutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Borders(7).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(7).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(8).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(8).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(9).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(9).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(11).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(11).Weight = 2
	Excel.Selection.Borders(12).LineStyle = 1
	Excel.Selection.Borders(12).Weight = 2
	Sh1.Cells(satirBasla, 1).Select
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Function GetSipArr(MPSNo)
	Dim SipArr(4)
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dwrw3gtt43", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select2("d35355", M10TM.GetAppName, "MUSTERISIPINO<>'          '", "", -1)
	
	M10TM1.GroupBy("MUSTERISIPINO")
	For i = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
		SipArr(0) = SipArr(0) & ", " & trim(M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(i))
		CHSipNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(i), "")
		if trim(CHSipNo) <> "" Then
			SipArr(1) = SipArr(1) & ", " & trim(CHSipNo)
		End if
	Next
	if trim(SipArr(0)) <> "" Then SipArr(0) = Mid(SipArr(0), 3)
	if trim(SipArr(1)) <> "" Then SipArr(1) = Mid(SipArr(1), 3)
	
	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select2("d35355", M10TM.GetAppName, "MUSTERIKODU<>'          '", "", -1)
	M10TM1.GroupBy("MUSTERIKODU")
	For i = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
		SipArr(2) = SipArr(2) & ", " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M10TM1.MUSTERIKODU(i), ""))
	Next
	if trim(SipArr(2)) <> "" Then SipArr(2) = Mid(SipArr(2), 3)
	
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		if trim(M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)) <> "" Then
			SipArr(3) = SipArr(3) + GetSipeRezerveMik(M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i))
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("fsw46yy", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			SipArr(4) = SipArr(4) + S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - S40T.SF_SEVKDEMIK
		End if
	Next
	GetSipArr = SipArr
End Function
Function FormatDeger(Deger)
	Dim DegerArray(1)
	Deger = Replace(Deger, "[", "")
	Deger = Replace(Deger, "]", "")
	DegerArr = Split(Deger, "-")
	if uBound(DegerArr) = 2 Then
		DegerArr(0) = Replace(DegerArr(0), ",", "")
		DegerArr(1) = Replace(DegerArr(1), ",", "")
		YMMiktar = Doc.CDbl(DegerArr(0))
		TamamlananMiktar = Doc.CDbl(DegerArr(1))
		if TamamlananMiktar >= YMMiktar Then
			DegerArray(1) = 36
		Elseif TamamlananMiktar > 0 Then
			DegerArray(1) = 35
		Else
			DegerArray(1) = 0
		End if
		
		DegerArr(0) = Doc.NumFormat0(YMMiktar / 1000, 12, 1)
		DegerArr(1) = Doc.NumFormat0(TamamlananMiktar / 1000, 12, 1)
		
		DegerArr(0) = TurkceFormat(DegerArr(0))
		DegerArr(1) = TurkceFormat(DegerArr(1))
		
		DegerArray(0) = DegerArr(1) & "/" & DegerArr(0)
	End if
	FormatDeger = DegerArray
End Function
Sub NumaraFormatla(Excel, Sh1, Sutun, BaslangicSatir, BitisSatir)
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(BaslangicSatir, Sutun), Sh1.Cells(BitisSatir, Sutun)).Select
	Excel.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.0"
End Sub
Function TurkceFormat(SayiStr)
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ",", ";")
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ".", ",")
	SayiStr = Replace(SayiStr, ";", ".")
	TurkceFormat = SayiStr
End Function
Function GetSipeRezerveMik(SipArtNo)
	GetSipeRezerveMik = 0
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("we356tg3w4gw", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & SipArtNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	S01.GroupBy("KOD")
	GetSipeRezerveMik = S01.SF_MIKTAR
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MKR
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MAKARA ETÝKETÝ
Kod : 
Dim SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, ustMargin, solMargin, altMargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, satirNo, Gen, Gen2, Standart
Dim MPSNo, StokKodu, MusteriCHKodu, Surtel, birim, NetA, MakaraTuru, MakaraAg, MusteriSipNo, UretimTarihi,makarano
Dim SS40T, S20, S40E, MPS10, Etkt, EtiketEni, UrunAdi

sub printform()
	STOK20T.safepack
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni = 210
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	EtiketEni = 56
	altMargin = ustMargin + 24 * satirYuksekligi
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satirYuksekligi =10
	Gen = 40
	Gen2 = 95
	Standart = ""
	ust = 1
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"PALET ETÝKETÝ" & trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call sayfaBasi(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.show()
	
end sub


'************************************************************************************************************

Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)
	
	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "SF_VRI_VR_R1<>'" & space(24) & "'", "",-1)
	Doc.runmacro1 "PFRM7S.PALET","printform1", pe1
	' Palet numarasý verilenleri getir
	
	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "", "",-1)
	
	for i = 1 to S20.GetRecCount
		
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþ baðlantýlarý hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & S20.GetRecCount
		
		MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
		M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		LotNo = S20.LOTNUMBER(i)
		AmbBoy = S20.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		
		Set MPS10 = Doc.Select1("A9Bc", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MPSNo)&"' & TRNUM='"&trim(M_ArtNo)&"'", "",-1)
		
		MusteriSipNo = MPS10.MUSTERISIPINO
		
		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("PAdBc", "STOK40E", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MusteriSipNo)&"'", "",-1)
		
		MusteriCHKodu = S40E.MUSTERIKODU
		MusteriSipNoORD = S40E.CHSIPNO
		FaturaTip = S40E.MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK
		FTip = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("*CHHNT","KOD",FaturaTip,-1))
		SiparisTarihi = S40E.TARIH
		
		S_ARTNO = MPS10.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
		S_ARTNO = TRIM(MusteriSipNo)+S_ARTNO
		
		Qstr2 = ""
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "ARTNO='"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + S_ARTNO
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "'"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + ""
		
		Set SS40T = Doc.Select1("A90LFDSBC0xJKHSc", "STOK40T", Qstr2, "",-1)
		
		AmbTip = SS40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT
		
		S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(AmbBoy,10)
		S20.X_ALC_SIPARTNO(i) = MusteriCHKodu
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = AmbTip
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = FTip
		S20.D_ID(i) = MusteriSipNoORD
		pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		
		'  Call SendTableToExcel(S20)
		
		if AmbTip = "MAKARA" Then
			if trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8O002" then
				Call Oman(pe1, SS40T, i)
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8Y001" then
				solMargin =10
				satirYuksekligi =9
				Call Yadu(pe1, SS40T, i)
				satirYuksekligi =10
				solMargin =10
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8C001" then
				solMargin =10
				Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			elseif Trim(FTip) = "XF" then
				solMargin =10
				Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			End if
		End if
		
	Next
	
End Sub

'************************************************************************************************************

Sub Oman(pe1, SS40T,i)
	
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	'AMBLJ00.STOK_KODU_STOK00_AD
	
	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK
	
	' MSGBOX(MakaraAg)
	' MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	
	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg
	
	Set SS0GT = Doc.Select1("AGT111", "STOK0GT", "KOD='"& Trim(MusteriCHKodu) &"' & STOKCODE='"& Trim(StokKodu) &"'","",-1)
	
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = SS0GT.MSTOKCODE
	
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Stndrt = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	
	If pe1.getright > 10 then Pe1.PageBreak()
	
	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)
	
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	for m=1 to 2
		res = pe1.box(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*12, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 2 to 10
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 11 to 12
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*12, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*10, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)
		
		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi-1, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"THE SHIPPING M.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P.O.NO. & DATE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD&" DT: "&(Doc.StrToNorDate(SiparisTarihi))))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DESTINATION")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Adres))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"YEAR OF MFC")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Yil))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"SPEC NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Stndrt))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"ITEM CODE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunItem))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
		'  res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM SR.NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CABLE LENGTH (m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))
		
		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
			Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if
		
		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*11+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(BrutAg))
		
		ustMargin = 10
		solmargin = solMargin*2 + Gen + Gen2
	next
End Sub

'***********************************************************************

Sub Cleveland(pe1, SS40T,i)
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
	
	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	Surtel = "SURTEL CABLE CO./ISTANBUL-TURKEY"
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	
	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK
	
	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg
	
	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)
	
	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)
	
	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin*2 and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()
	' ElseIf pe1.getright > 10 then
	'  Pe1.PageBreak()
	' End if
	
	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If
	
	ust = 10
	satir = 1
	for y = 1 to 2
		
		res = pe1.box(ust, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*9, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 1 to 6
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 7 to 8
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next
		
		res = pe1.line(ust, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*9, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*6, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)
		
		res = pe1.write(ust+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,(Surtel))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CUSTOMER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P/O.NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi) & " " & Yil)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"STANDART")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Standart))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"LENGTH(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))
		
		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
			Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if
		
		res = pe1.write(ust+satirYuksekligi*7, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2, trim(BrutAg))
		
		ust= ust+(satirYuksekligi*10)
	next
	
End Sub


'***********************************************************************
Sub Yadu(pe1, SS40T,i)
	
	
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
	UretimTarihi = STOK20E.TARIH
	makarano = S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
	' Gen = 35
	' Gen2 = 25
	
	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	
	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("dfe1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK
	
	satirYuksekligi = 6
	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg
	
	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)
	
	Set STGT= Doc.Select1("01457", "STOK0GT", "KOD='"& Trim(MusteriCHKodu) &"'","",-1)
	
	for k=1 to STGT.GetRecCount
		if STGT.STOKCODE(k) = StokKodu then itemkod = STGT.MSTOKCODE(k)
	Next
	
	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)
	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()
	
	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If
	
	ust = 10
	for y = 1 to 2
		
		UretimTarihi2 = right(UretimTarihi,2)+Mid(UretimTarihi,6,2)+Mid(UretimTarihi,3,2)
		
		res = pe1.nopen()
		Yadulogo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!YLOGO"))
		res = pe1.Picture(Ust, SolMargin, -2, 130, YADUlogo, 114)
		
		res = pe1.setalign("Center")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",16)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ust+20, solMargin+1, 8, 130,"CABLE PN : " & trim(itemkod))
		
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",14)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, 130, trim(UrunAdi))
		
		Gen1 = 35
		Gen11 = 33
		Gen21 = 35
		Gen22 = 40

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
		DrumNo = Right("0000" & Doc.Sayac0danAl("YADU" & Mid(Replace(STOK20E.TARIH, "/", ""), 3)), 4)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen1,"BATCH NUMBER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen11, ": " & trim(UretimTarihi2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen21,"CABLE LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen22,": " & trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))&"(MT)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen1,"ORDER NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen11,": " & trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen21,"CABLE WEIGHT")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen22, ": " & trim(BrutAg) & " KG")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen1,"DRUM NO")
		'  res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, 30,": " & UCase(trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i))))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen11, ": " & DrumNo)
		
		
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen1,"FROM LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen11,": " & "0"&"(MT)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen21,"TO LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen22,": " & trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))&"(MT)")
		
		'  res = pe1.Print(pe1.GetBottom+10, SOLMARGIN,"RoHS")
		RoHSResim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "images\ROHS.JPG"
		res = pe1.Picture(pe1.GetBottom+4, SOLMARGIN, 8, -2, RoHSResim, 114)
		res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
		
		'  res = pe1.BOX(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight+3, 5, 5)
		
		Surtel = "S"
		'  Makarano = "J14102263"
		YaduBarkod = "88" & UretimTarihi2 & Surtel & DrumNo & itemkod
		
		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
			'    Etkt = EtiketYaz1(pe1,S20,i)
			res = pe1.setalign("Center")
			res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
			' res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 25, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")
			res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop, SOLMARGIN + 30, 15, 68, "CODE128", trim(yadubarkod), "")
			res = pe1.UseSet(1)
			
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if
		
		ust = ust+(satirYuksekligi*11)+35
	next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	
	call Normal(pe1)
	
	
	
End Sub



'************************************************************************************************************

Function MyNorDate(Tarih)
	MyNorDate = Right(Tarih, 2) & "." & Mid(Tarih, 6, 2) & "." & Left(Tarih, 4)
End Function

'*******************************************************************************************

Function EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
	
	' Set S20TS = Doc.Select2("A5Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
	' 'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik
	
	skod = S20.KOD(i)
	sipno = S20.X_ALC_SIPNO(i)
	Atip = S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
	ambBoy = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	birim = S20.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	stokkod = S20.KOD(i)
	MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
	Paletno = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
	Lotno = S20.MPSNO(i)
	Serino = S20.SERINO(i)
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S20.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)
	
	Olusturmatarihi = D7LB.CREA_TARIH
	
	' res = pe1.NoPen()
	' logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	' res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
	' res = pe1.NoPen()
	
	' res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi
	
	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 1
	sol = solmargin + Gen + Gen2 - EtiketEni
	
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	' res = pe1.Box(ust, sol, 11, EtiketEni)
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, EtiketEni - 2, trim(MamulAdi))
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.Write(ust, sol+5, 6, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(ust, (sol+EtiketEni) - 31, 6, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	' res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	' res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 6, sol + 5, 21, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	
End Function

'*******************************************************************************************

Function EtiketYaz1(pe1,S20,i)
	
	' Set S20TS = Doc.Select2("A5Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
	' 'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik
	
	skod = S20.KOD(i)
	sipno = S20.X_ALC_SIPNO(i)
	Atip = S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
	ambBoy = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	birim = S20.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	stokkod = S20.KOD(i)
	MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
	Paletno = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
	Lotno = S20.MPSNO(i)
	Serino = S20.SERINO(i)
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S20.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)
	
	Olusturmatarihi = D7LB.CREA_TARIH
	
	' res = pe1.NoPen()
	' logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	' res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
	' res = pe1.NoPen()
	
	' res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi
	
	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 1
	sol = solmargin + Gen + Gen2 - EtiketEni
	
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	' res = pe1.Box(ust, sol, 11, EtiketEni)
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, EtiketEni - 2, trim(MamulAdi))
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	' res = pe1.Write(ust, sol+5, 6, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	' res = pe1.Write(ust, (sol+EtiketEni) - 31, 6, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	' res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	' res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))
	
	' UretimTarihi = STOK20E.TARIH
	' makarano
	' itemkod
	' MSGBOX UretimTarihi
	' MSGBOX makarano
	' MSGBOX itemkod
	
	YaduBarkod = 88 + UretimTarihi + S + makarano + itemkod
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	' res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 8, solmargin + 10, 19, Gen*2, "CODE128", trim(YaduBarkod), "")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	
End Function

'************************************************************************************************************

Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub



'************************************************************************************************************


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MKR-PLT-E1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MAKARA ETÝKETÝ
Kod : 
Dim SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, ustMargin, solMargin, altMargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, satirNo, Gen, Gen2, Standart
Dim MPSNo, StokKodu, MusteriCHKodu, Surtel, birim, NetA, MakaraTuru, MakaraAg, MusteriSipNo, UretimTarihi,makarano
Dim SS40T, S20, S40E, MPS10, Etkt, EtiketEni, UrunAdi

sub printform()
	STOK20T.safepack
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni = 210
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	EtiketEni = 56
	altMargin = ustMargin + 24 * satirYuksekligi
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satirYuksekligi =10
	Gen = 40
	Gen2 = 95
	Standart = ""
	ust = 1

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"PALET ETÝKETÝ" & trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call sayfaBasi(pe1)

	res = pe1.show()

end sub


'************************************************************************************************************

Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)

	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "SF_VRI_VR_R1<>'" & space(24) & "'", "",-1)
			Doc.runmacro1 "PFRM7S.PALET","printform1", pe1
	'	Palet numarasý verilenleri getir

	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "", "",-1)

	for i = 1 to S20.GetRecCount

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþ baðlantýlarý hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & S20.GetRecCount

		MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
		M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		LotNo = S20.LOTNUMBER(i)
		AmbBoy = S20.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		Set MPS10 = Doc.Select1("A9Bc", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MPSNo)&"' & TRNUM='"&trim(M_ArtNo)&"'", "",-1)

		MusteriSipNo = MPS10.MUSTERISIPINO

		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("PAdBc", "STOK40E", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MusteriSipNo)&"'", "",-1)

		MusteriCHKodu = S40E.MUSTERIKODU
		MusteriSipNoORD = S40E.CHSIPNO
		FaturaTip = S40E.MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK
		FTip = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("*CHHNT","KOD",FaturaTip,-1))
		SiparisTarihi = S40E.TARIH

		S_ARTNO = MPS10.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
		S_ARTNO = TRIM(MusteriSipNo)+S_ARTNO

		Qstr2 = ""
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "ARTNO='"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + S_ARTNO
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "'"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + ""

		Set SS40T = Doc.Select1("A90LFDSBC0xJKHSc", "STOK40T", Qstr2, "",-1)

		AmbTip = SS40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT

		S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(AmbBoy,10)
		S20.X_ALC_SIPARTNO(i) = MusteriCHKodu
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = AmbTip
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = FTip
		S20.D_ID(i) = MusteriSipNoORD
		pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

'		Call SendTableToExcel(S20)

		if AmbTip = "MAKARA" Then
			if trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8O002" then
					Call Oman(pe1, SS40T, i)
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8Y001" then
				solMargin =10
				satirYuksekligi =9
					Call Yadu(pe1, SS40T, i)
				satirYuksekligi =10
				solMargin =10
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8C001" then
				solMargin =10
					Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			elseif Trim(FTip) = "XF" then
				solMargin =10
					Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			End if
		End if

	Next

End Sub

'************************************************************************************************************

Sub Oman(pe1, SS40T,i)

	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
'AMBLJ00.STOK_KODU_STOK00_AD

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

'	MSGBOX(MakaraAg)
'	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)

	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Set SS0GT = Doc.Select1("AGT111", "STOK0GT", "KOD='"& Trim(MusteriCHKodu) &"' & STOKCODE='"& Trim(StokKodu) &"'","",-1)

	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = SS0GT.MSTOKCODE

	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Stndrt = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")

	If pe1.getright > 10 then Pe1.PageBreak()
	
	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)
	
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	for m=1 to 2
			res = pe1.box(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*12, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 2 to 10
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 11 to 12
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*12, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*10, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)

		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi-1, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"THE SHIPPING M.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P.O.NO. & DATE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD&" DT: "&(Doc.StrToNorDate(SiparisTarihi))))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DESTINATION")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Adres))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"YEAR OF MFC")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Yil))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"SPEC NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Stndrt))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"ITEM CODE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunItem))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
'		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM SR.NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CABLE LENGTH (m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
				Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if

		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*11+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(BrutAg))
		
		ustMargin = 10
		solmargin = solMargin*2 + Gen + Gen2
	next
End Sub

'***********************************************************************

Sub Cleveland(pe1, SS40T,i)
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)

 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	Surtel = "SURTEL CABLE CO./ISTANBUL-TURKEY"
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)

	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)

	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin*2 and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()
'	ElseIf pe1.getright > 10 then 
'		Pe1.PageBreak()
'	End if

	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If

	ust = 10
	satir = 1
	for y = 1 to 2

			res = pe1.box(ust, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*9, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 1 to 6
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 7 to 8
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next

		res = pe1.line(ust, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*9, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*6, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)

		res = pe1.write(ust+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,(Surtel))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CUSTOMER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P/O.NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi) & " " & Yil)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"STANDART")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Standart))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"LENGTH(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
				Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if

		res = pe1.write(ust+satirYuksekligi*7, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2, trim(BrutAg))

		ust= ust+(satirYuksekligi*10)
	next
	
End Sub


'***********************************************************************
Sub Yadu(pe1, SS40T,i)


	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
	UretimTarihi = STOK20E.TARIH
	makarano = S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
'	Gen = 35
'	Gen2 = 25

 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("dfe1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)

	Set STGT= Doc.Select1("01457", "STOK0GT", "KOD='"& Trim(MusteriCHKodu) &"'","",-1)

	for k=1 to STGT.GetRecCount
		if STGT.STOKCODE(k) = StokKodu then itemkod = STGT.MSTOKCODE(k)
	Next

	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)
	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()

	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If
	
	ust = 10
	for y = 1 to 2

		UretimTarihi2 = right(UretimTarihi,2)+Mid(UretimTarihi,6,2)+Mid(UretimTarihi,3,2)

	res = pe1.nopen()
	Yadulogo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!YLOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust, SolMargin+10, 15, 130, YADUlogo, 114)

	res = pe1.setalign("Center")
		res = pe1.write(ust+20, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, 130,"CABLE PN :" & trim(itemkod))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, 130, trim(UrunAdi))
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"BATCH NUMBER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,trim(UretimTarihi2))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CABLE LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))&"(MT)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"ORDER NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CABLE WEIGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen, trim(BrutAg)&"(KG)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM NO :")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,UCase(trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i))))

		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"FROM LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"0"&"(MT)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"TO LENGTH")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))&"(MT)")

		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+5, SOLMARGIN, satirYuksekligi, 10,"RoHS")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

		res = pe1.BOX(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight+3, 5, 5)

		Surtel = "S"
		Makarano = "J14102263"
		YaduBarkod = 88 & UretimTarihi2 & Surtel & makarano & itemkod

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
'				Etkt = EtiketYaz1(pe1,S20,i)
	res = pe1.setalign("Center")
					res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
					res = pe1.NoPen()
					res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
				'	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")
					res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop-5, SOLMARGIN + 25, 20, Gen*2, "CODE128", trim(yadubarkod), "")
					res = pe1.UseSet(1)

			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if
		
		ust = ust+(satirYuksekligi*11)
	next

call Normal(pe1)
	

	
End Sub



'************************************************************************************************************

Function MyNorDate(Tarih)
	MyNorDate = Right(Tarih, 2) & "." & Mid(Tarih, 6, 2) & "." & Left(Tarih, 4)
End Function

'*******************************************************************************************

Function EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)

'	Set S20TS = Doc.Select2("A5Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
'	'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik

	skod = S20.KOD(i)
	sipno = S20.X_ALC_SIPNO(i)
	Atip = S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
	ambBoy = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	birim = S20.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	stokkod = S20.KOD(i)
	MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
	Paletno = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
	Lotno = S20.MPSNO(i)
	Serino = S20.SERINO(i)

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S20.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)

	Olusturmatarihi = D7LB.CREA_TARIH

'	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
'	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
'	res = pe1.NoPen()

'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi

	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 1
	sol = solmargin + Gen + Gen2 - EtiketEni

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
'	res = pe1.Box(ust, sol, 11, EtiketEni)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, EtiketEni - 2, trim(MamulAdi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.Write(ust, sol+5, 6, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(ust, (sol+EtiketEni) - 31, 6, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
'	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")	
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 6, sol + 5, 21, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Function

'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi

	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 1

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
'	res = pe1.Write(ust, sol+5, 6, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
'	res = pe1.Write(ust, (sol+EtiketEni) - 31, 6, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))

'	itemkod

	YaduBarkod = 88 + UretimTarihi + S + makarano + itemkod

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Function

'************************************************************************************************************

Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub



'************************************************************************************************************


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
