Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MKR-PLT-ET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MAKARA ETÝKETÝ
Kod : 
Dim SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, ustMargin, solMargin, altMargin, sagMargin, satirYuksekligi, satirNo, Gen, Gen2, Standart
Dim MPSNo, StokKodu, MusteriCHKodu, Surtel, birim, NetA, MakaraTuru, MakaraAg, MusteriSipNo
Dim SS40T, S20, S40E, MPS10, Etkt, EtiketEni, UrunAdi

sub printform()
	STOK20T.safepack
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni = 210
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	EtiketEni = 56
	altMargin = ustMargin + 24 * satirYuksekligi
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satirYuksekligi =10
	Gen = 40
	Gen2 = 95
	Standart = ""
	ust = 1

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"PALET ETÝKETÝ" & trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call sayfaBasi(pe1)

	res = pe1.show()

end sub


'************************************************************************************************************

Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)

	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "SF_VRI_VR_R1<>'" & space(24) & "'", "",-1)
			Doc.runmacro1 "PFRM7S.PALET","printform1", pe1
	'	Palet numarasý verilenleri getir

	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc", "STOK20T", "", "",-1)

	for i = 1 to S20.GetRecCount

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþ baðlantýlarý hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & S20.GetRecCount

		MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
		M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		LotNo = S20.LOTNUMBER(i)
		AmbBoy = S20.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		Set MPS10 = Doc.Select1("A9Bc", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MPSNo)&"' & TRNUM='"&trim(M_ArtNo)&"'", "",-1)

		MusteriSipNo = MPS10.MUSTERISIPINO

		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("PAdBc", "STOK40E", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MusteriSipNo)&"'", "",-1)

		MusteriCHKodu = S40E.MUSTERIKODU
		MusteriSipNoORD = S40E.CHSIPNO
		FaturaTip = S40E.MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK
		FTip = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("*CHHNT","KOD",FaturaTip,-1))
		SiparisTarihi = S40E.TARIH

		S_ARTNO = MPS10.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
		S_ARTNO = TRIM(MusteriSipNo)+S_ARTNO

		Qstr2 = ""
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "ARTNO='"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + S_ARTNO
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "'"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + ""

		Set SS40T = Doc.Select1("A90LFDSBC0xJKHSc", "STOK40T", Qstr2, "",-1)

		AmbTip = SS40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT

		S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(AmbBoy,10)
		S20.X_ALC_SIPARTNO(i) = MusteriCHKodu
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = AmbTip
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = FTip
		S20.D_ID(i) = MusteriSipNoORD
		pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

'		Call SendTableToExcel(S20)

		if AmbTip = "MAKARA" Then
			if trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8O002" then
					Call Oman(pe1, SS40T, i)
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8Y001" then
				solMargin =10
				satirYuksekligi =9
					Call Yadu(pe1, SS40T, i)
				satirYuksekligi =10
				solMargin =10
			elseif trim(MusteriCHKodu) = "8C001" then
				solMargin =10
					Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			elseif Trim(FTip) = "XF" then
				solMargin =10
					Call Cleveland(pe1, SS40T, i)
				solMargin =10
			End if
		End if

	Next

End Sub

'************************************************************************************************************

Sub Oman(pe1, SS40T,i)

	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
'AMBLJ00.STOK_KODU_STOK00_AD

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

'	MSGBOX(MakaraAg)
'	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)

	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Set SS0GT = Doc.Select1("AGT111", "STOK0GT", "KOD='"& Trim(MusteriCHKodu) &"' & STOKCODE='"& Trim(StokKodu) &"'","",-1)

	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = SS0GT.MSTOKCODE

	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Stndrt = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")

	If pe1.getright > 10 then Pe1.PageBreak()
	
	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)
	
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 10
	for m=1 to 2
			res = pe1.box(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*12, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 2 to 10
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 11 to 12
			res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*12, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*10, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)

		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*1+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi-1, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"THE SHIPPING M.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P.O.NO. & DATE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD&" DT: "&(Doc.StrToNorDate(SiparisTarihi))))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DESTINATION")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Adres))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"YEAR OF MFC")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Yil))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"SPEC NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Stndrt))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"ITEM CODE")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunItem))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
'		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM SR.NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CABLE LENGTH (m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
				Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if

		res = pe1.write(ustMargin+satirYuksekligi*11+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT (kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-EtiketEni,trim(BrutAg))
		
		ustMargin = 10
		solmargin = solMargin*2 + Gen + Gen2
	next
End Sub

'***********************************************************************

Sub Cleveland(pe1, SS40T,i)
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)

 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	Surtel = "SURTEL CABLE CO./ISTANBUL-TURKEY"
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)

	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)

	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin*2 and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()
'	ElseIf pe1.getright > 10 then 
'		Pe1.PageBreak()
'	End if

	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If

	ust = 10
	satir = 1
	for y = 1 to 2

			res = pe1.box(ust, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*9, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 1 to 6
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 7 to 8
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next

		res = pe1.line(ust, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*9, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*6, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)

		res = pe1.write(ust+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MANUFACTURER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,(Surtel))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CUSTOMER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P/O.NO.")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(UrunAdi) & " " & Yil)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"STANDART")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Standart))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"LENGTH(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
				Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if

		res = pe1.write(ust+satirYuksekligi*7, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"NET WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(NetA))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGHT(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.Gettop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2, trim(BrutAg))

		ust= ust+(satirYuksekligi*10)
	next
	
End Sub


'***********************************************************************
Sub Yadu(pe1, SS40T,i)
	MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
	M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
	StokKodu = S20.KOD(i)
	MusteriSipNoORD = S20.D_ID(i)
	MakaraTuru = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)

 	UrunAdi = doc.GetUDFValue("05",stokKodu,"EN_AD",0)
	MakaraAg = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","B_AGIRLIK",MakaraTuru,"")
	MusteriAd = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	SiparisTarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","TARIH",MusteriSipNo,"")
	UrunItem = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT","MSTOKCODE",StokKodu,"")
	Adres = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",MusteriCHKodu,"")
	Yil = Left(SiparisTarihi, 4)
	Standart = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","GK_7",StokKodu,"")
	uzunluk = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

	Set AMBLJ0= Doc.Select1("00AGT11122K1", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='"& Trim(MakaraTuru) &"'","",-1)
	MakaraAg = AMBLJ0.AGIRLIK

	NetA = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG",StokKodu,uzunluk)
	NetA = Doc.Round(NetA,0)
	NetA = NetA-(Doc.Fmod(NetA,5))
	MakaraAg = Doc.Round(MakaraAg,0)
	MakaraAg = MakaraAg-(Doc.Fmod(MakaraAg,5))
	BrutAg = NetA + MakaraAg

	Qstr2 = "CH_KODU = (MusteriCHKodu) & EVRAKNO = (StokKodu)"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("ABClklgfxck", "STOK0A", Qstr2,"",-1)

	sagsutun = (solMargin + Gen + Gen2 + 5)
	If pe1.gettop > ustmargin and pe1.getright > (sagsutun-EtiketEni)*2 then Pe1.PageBreak()

	if pe1.getright < sagsutun Then
		solMargin = solMargin + 5 + Gen + Gen2
	End If
	
	ust = 10
	for y = 1 to 2

	res = pe1.nopen()
	YADUlogo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!YLOGO"))
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust, SolMargin, 15, Gen+Gen2, YADUlogo, 114)

			res = pe1.box(ust, solMargin, satirYuksekligi*10, Gen+Gen2)
		for satir = 1 to 7
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, Gen+Gen2)
		next
		for satir = 8 to 9
			res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*satir, solMargin, 0, (Gen+Gen2)-EtiketEni)
		next

		res = pe1.line(ust, solMargin + Gen, satirYuksekligi*10, 0)
		res = pe1.line(ust+satirYuksekligi*7, solMargin + Gen+Gen2-EtiketEni, satirYuksekligi*3, 0)

		res = pe1.write(ust+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"CUSTOMER")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriAd))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DESTINATION")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Adres))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"MATERIAL")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2, trim(UrunAdi) & " " & Yil)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"P/O.NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(MusteriSipNoORD))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"STANDART")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(Standart))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"DRUM NO")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"LENGTH(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"GROSS WEIGTH(kg)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2, trim(BrutAg))

		if trim(S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" then
			res=pe1.storeset(18)
				Etkt = EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)
			res=pe1.useset(18)
		End if

		res = pe1.write(ust+satirYuksekligi*8+1, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"FROM(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,"0")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom, solMargin+1, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"TO(m)")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop, pe1.getRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen2,trim(S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)))
		
		ust = ust+(satirYuksekligi*11)
	next
	
	
End Sub



'************************************************************************************************************

Function MyNorDate(Tarih)
	MyNorDate = Right(Tarih, 2) & "." & Mid(Tarih, 6, 2) & "." & Left(Tarih, 4)
End Function

'*******************************************************************************************

Function EtiketYaz(pe1,S20,i)

'	Set S20TS = Doc.Select2("A5Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
'	'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik

	skod = S20.KOD(i)
	sipno = S20.X_ALC_SIPNO(i)
	Atip = S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
	ambBoy = S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
	birim = S20.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	stokkod = S20.KOD(i)
	MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
	Paletno = S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
	Lotno = S20.MPSNO(i)
	Serino = S20.SERINO(i)

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S20.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)

	Olusturmatarihi = D7LB.CREA_TARIH

'	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
'	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
'	res = pe1.NoPen()

'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi

	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 1
	sol = solmargin + Gen + Gen2 - EtiketEni

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
'	res = pe1.Box(ust, sol, 11, EtiketEni)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, EtiketEni - 2, trim(MamulAdi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.Write(ust, sol+5, 6, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(ust, (sol+EtiketEni) - 31, 6, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
'	res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
'	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")	
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 6, sol + 5, 21, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Function

'************************************************************************************************************


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MMPS10-SR
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Surtel Ýþ Emri
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, M10TI, M10TH
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, hmdSatirBasla, MuhendislikSatirBasla
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic

Sub PrintForm()
	if trim(MMPS10E.STATUS) <> "R" Then
		MMPS10E.STATUS = "R"
	End if

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 148.5
	
	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 5
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 10

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri - " & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-14)

	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("sese222", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call IsEmriPrint(pe1, i)
	Next
	satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom()
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	if trim(MMPS10E.REBTARIH_OFO) = "" Then MMPS10E.REBTARIH_OFO = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun)
	MMPS10E.REETARIH_OFO = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun)
	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Sub IsEmriPrint(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)

	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("de3434r", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & M10TI.R_SIRANO(OpSatirNo) & "'", "", -1) 
	siraNo = 0        
	Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpsatirNo)
	For i = 1 To M10TH.GetRowCount()
		Call HammaddeYaz(pe1, i, OpSatirNo)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	MamulMiktari = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MamulMiktari)
	MamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(MamulMiktari,18,Ondalik)
	StokBirimi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,"")

	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	A = doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_1",MusteriKodu,"")
	MusteriAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK1","AD",A,-1)
	Not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	Not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2

	TezgahKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	Tezgahlar = trim(TezgahKodu)
	AlternatifTezgahlar = GetAlternatifTezgah(M10TI.R_MAMULKODU(OpSatirNo), M10TI.ALTERNATIFKODU(OpSatirNo))
	if trim(AlternatifTezgahlar) <> "" Then
		Tezgahlar = Tezgahlar & " (" & AlternatifTezgahlar & ")"
	End if
	TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",TezgahKodu,"")
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,"")
	MamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_MPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMPaketIcerigi = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(OpSatirNo)
	YMMiktari = M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo)

	YMPaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(YMPaketIcerigi,18,6)

	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin, 6,180,FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Gen10 = 32
	Gen11 = 48
	Gen20 = 26

	GrupNo = MMPS10E.GRUPEVRAKNO
	ToleransTipi = MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE
	Tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS

	bBasla = UstMargin
	SatirAra = 6
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin, 6, sagMargin - solmargin, trim(OperasyonAdi) & " Ýþ Emri")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Tezgah")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & Tezgahlar)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Termin")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrtoNorDate(MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH))

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)) & "/" & Doc.CDbl(M10TI.TRNUM(OpSatirNo)))

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Operasyon No")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)) & "/" & Doc.CDbl(M10TI.TRNUM(OpSatirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri Miktarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo),18,0)) & " " & trim(MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	if Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 1) = "3" Then
		MamulYarimamul = "Mamul"
	Else
		MamulYarimamul = "Yarýmamul"
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Kodu")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)
	AdSol = pe1.GetLeft()

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))
	StokAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	if (Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) = "203" or Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 4) = "2001") and trim(StokAdi2) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, AdSol + 1, 6, SagMargin - AdSol - 1,"(" & trim(StokAdi2) & ")")
	End if

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Operasyon Adý")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & OperasyonAdi)

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Tezgah")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & trim(TezgahKodu))

	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

	TMBasla = 0
	if trim(MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(1)) = "UP" and Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "202" Then
		Gen1 = 72
		Gen2 = 25
		Gen4 = 30

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kaynak YarýMamul/Mamul")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kaynak MPS")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kaynak MPS Mik.")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 1
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra

		MMPS10TM.SafePack()
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirYuksekligi
			KaynakStokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", KaynakStokKodu, ""))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, trim(MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4-2, Doc.NumFormat(MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), 12, 0))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Next
		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 1, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
	End if

	if Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "202" Then
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
		Gen1 = 30
		Gen2 = 23
		Gen3 = 25
		Gen4 = 30
		Gen5 = 20

		if TMBasla = 0 Then
			Basla1 = pe1.GetTop + satirAra
		Else
			Basla1 = pe1.GetBottom + 3
		End if
		par1 = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
		par2 = ""
		Set ML = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetKaynakMPSList", par1, par2)
		Set ML1 = Doc.Select2("dfr5t456", ML.GetAppName, "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='SP'","",-1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(Basla1, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Müþteri")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen2, "Sipariþ No")
		if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen3, "Müþteri Sip. No")
		End if
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen4, "Sevk Boyu")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen5, "Teslim Tarihi")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 1
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to ML1.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirYuksekligi
			CHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("wd3dfsdf", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i) & "'&TRNUM='" & ML1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
			SipPaketMiktari = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR(1)
			SipPaketIcerigi = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR(1)
			SipPaketIcerigiStr = trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(SipPaketIcerigi, 12,6))
			if trim(S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) <> "" Then
				SipPaketIcerigiStr = SipPaketIcerigiStr & " " & trim(S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) & " % " & trim(trim(S40T1.TOLERANS(1)))
			End if
			SipTeslimTarihi = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH(1)
			res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1 - 2, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, ""))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Gen2, trim(ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
			if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen3, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")))
			End if
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen4, SipPaketMiktari & " x " & SipPaketIcerigiStr)
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen5, Doc.StrToNorDate(SipTeslimTarihi))
		Next
		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 1, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		End if
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen4, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End if
	hmdSatirBasla = Alt + satirYuksekligi
	MuhendislikSatirBasla = Alt + 2

End Sub
Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	YariMamulKodu = M10TI.R_YMAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = M10TI.R_KAYNAKTYPE(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
	if Tech.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
'	Tech.sort(">TSCODE")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(MuhendislikSatirBasla, solMargin, 5, 50, "Teknik Özellikler")
	MuhSatirBasla = MuhendislikSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi + 0.5
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Gen1 = 50
	res = pe1.Write(MuhSatirBasla, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Parametre")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Deðer")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	For i = 1 To Tech.GetRecCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			satirPozr = MuhSatirBasla + Satiryuksekligi
		Else
			satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom + 1
		End if
		if trim(Tech.TSCODE(i)) = "RESIM" Then
			DosyaAdi = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\" & trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i))
			sol = solMargin + Gen1 + 1
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, sol, -2, sagMargin - sol - 1, DosyaAdi, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i)))
		End if
	Next
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = sagMargin
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(MuhSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
	hmdSatirBasla = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi - 0.4, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla -1, solMargin + Gen1, KutuAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, 0)
End Sub
Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, satirNo, OpSatirNo)
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi

	HammaddeKodu = M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)
	HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,"")
	HammaddeMiktari = M10TH.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo)	
	HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,"")
	Gen1 = 35
	Gen2 = 65
	Gen3 = 20
	Gen4 = 10
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	if satirNo = 1 Then
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1, "Hammadde Kodu")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen2, "Hammadde Adý")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen3, "Miktar")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 2, 5,Gen4, "Birim")	
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	End if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1, HammaddeKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen2, HammaddeAdi)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Ondalik = M10TH.MaxSigDig("R_MIKTAR0")
	if Ondalik > 3 Then Ondalik = 3
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen3, Doc.NumFormat(HammaddeMiktari, 12,Ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 2, 5,Gen4, HammaddeBirim)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	if satirNo = M10TH.GetRowCount() Then
		res = pe1.Box(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin + Gen1, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3 + 2, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1 + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.Write(Alt, SagMargin - 30,6,32," ")
End Sub
Sub Hazirlayan(pe1,SatirPozr)
	If (satirPozr + 30) > satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		satirPozr = UstMargin + 6
	End If
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, SolMargin, 6, (SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "HAZIRLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "ONAYLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 3, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 30,0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 12, SolMargin, 0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 3, SolMargin, 30,SagMargin-SolMargin)
End Sub
Function GetAlternatifTezgah(UrunKodu, AlternatifKodu)
	GetAlternatifTezgah = ""
	if trim(AlternatifKodu) = "" Then Exit Function
	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & UrunKodu & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & AlternatifKodu & "'"
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("dwgt4gtgyy", "BOMU01T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() <= 1 Then Exit Function

	For i = 2 to B01T.GetRowCount
		GetAlternatifTezgah = GetAlternatifTezgah & ", " & trim(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
	Next
	GetAlternatifTezgah = Mid(GetAlternatifTezgah, 3)
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MMPS10-SR1
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS301
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Surtel Ýþ Emri
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, M10TI, M10TH, M10E, M10TM
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, hmdSatirBasla, MuhendislikSatirBasla
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 148.5
	
	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 5
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 10

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Ýþ Emri", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-14)

	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to MMPS30T.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(trim(MMPS30T.SELECTED(i))) and trim(Doc.Getd7UserName()) <> "FX" Then
			if Not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
				Set M10E1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
				DocYaratildi = True
			End if
			Doc1.Load_Voucher MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i)
			if trim(MMPS30T.STATUS(i)) <> "R" Then M10E1.STATUS = "R"
			if trim(M10E1.REBTARIH_OFO) = "" Then M10E1.REBTARIH_OFO = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun)
			M10E1.REETARIH_OFO = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun)
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			if nret = 0 Then MMPS30T.STATUS(i) = "R"
		End if
	Next

	For i = 1 to MMPS30T.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(trim(MMPS30T.SELECTED(i))) Then
			
			Set M10E = Doc.Select1("esee434", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='" & MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			Set M10T = Doc.Select1("MMPS10T", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("cfftt566", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		
			Yazilan = Yazilan + 1
			if Yazilan > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
			Call PrintFomrm1(pe1, i)
		End if
	Next

'	Call SayfaSayisiYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub PrintFomrm1(pe1, satirNo)
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("sese222", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then pe1.PageBreak
		Call IsEmriPrint(pe1, i)
	Next
	satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom()
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
End Sub
Sub IsEmriPrint(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)

	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("de3434r", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_SIRANO='" & M10TI.R_SIRANO(OpSatirNo) & "'", "", -1) 
	siraNo = 0        
	Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpsatirNo)
	For i = 1 To M10TH.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 and (hmdSatirBasla + (M10TH.GetRowCount + 1) * satirYuksekligi) > AltMargin Then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			hmdSatirBasla = UstMargin
		End if 

		Call HammaddeYaz(pe1, i, OpSatirNo)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	res = pe1.nopen()
        
	MamulMiktari = Doc.Round(M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 0)
	Ondalik = Doc.SigDig(MamulMiktari)
	MamulKodu = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	MamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(MamulMiktari,18,Ondalik)
	StokBirimi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,"")

	MusteriKodu = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	A = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","GK_1",MusteriKodu,"")
	MusteriAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK1","AD",A,-1)
	Not1 = M10E.NOTLAR_1
	Not2 = M10E.NOTLAR_2

	TezgahKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	Tezgahlar = trim(TezgahKodu)
	AlternatifTezgahlar = GetAlternatifTezgah(M10TI.R_MAMULKODU(OpSatirNo), M10TI.ALTERNATIFKODU(OpSatirNo))
	if trim(AlternatifTezgahlar) <> "" Then
		Tezgahlar = Tezgahlar & " (" & AlternatifTezgahlar & ")"
	End if
	TezgahAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00","AD",TezgahKodu,"")
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,"")
	MamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_MPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMamulPaket = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKET(OpSatirNo)
	YMPaketIcerigi = M10TI.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(OpSatirNo)
	YMMiktari = M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo)
	YMPaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(YMPaketIcerigi,18,6)

	FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin, 6,180,FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Gen10 = 32
	Gen11 = 48
	Gen20 = 26

	GrupNo = M10E.GRUPEVRAKNO
	ToleransTipi = M10E.TOLERANSTYPE
	Tolerans = M10E.TOLERANS

	bBasla = UstMargin
	SatirAra = 6
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin, 6, sagMargin - solmargin, trim(OperasyonAdi) & " Ýþ Emri")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Tezgah")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & Tezgahlar)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Termin")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrtoNorDate(M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)) & "/" & Doc.CDbl(M10TI.TRNUM(OpSatirNo)))

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Operasyon No")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(M10TI.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)) & "/" & Doc.CDbl(M10TI.TRNUM(OpSatirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Ýþ Emri Miktarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(Doc.Round(M10TI.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(OpSatirNo),0),18,6)) & " " & trim(M10E.SF_SF_UNIT))

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen20, "Termin Tarihi")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrtoNorDate(M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH))

	if Left(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 1) = "3" Then
		MamulYarimamul = "Mamul"
	Else
		MamulYarimamul = "Yarýmamul"
	End if
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Kodu")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)
	AdSol = pe1.GetLeft()

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, MamulYarimamul & " Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, ""))

	StokAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	if (Left(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) = "203" or Left(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 4) = "2001") and trim(StokAdi2) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, AdSol + 1, 6, SagMargin - AdSol - 1,"(" & trim(StokAdi2) & ")")
	End if

'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Operasyon Adý")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & OperasyonAdi)

	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

	TMBasla = 0
	if trim(M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(1)) = "UP" and Left(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1), 3) <> "202" Then
		Gen1 = 72
		Gen2 = 25
		Gen4 = 30

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra + 1, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Kaynak YarýMamul/Mamul")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, "Kaynak MPS")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4, "Kaynak MPS Mik.")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 1
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra

		M10TM.SafePack()
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirYuksekligi
			KaynakStokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", KaynakStokKodu, ""))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen2, trim(M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, Gen4-2, Doc.NumFormat(M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), 12, 0))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Next
		Alt = pe1.GetBottom()

		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 1, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
		res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
	End if

	if Left(M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "202" Then
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 10)
		Gen1 = 30
		Gen2 = 23
		Gen3 = 25
		Gen4 = 30
		Gen5 = 20

		par1 = M10E.EVRAKNO
		par2 = ""
		Set ML = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetKaynakMPSList", par1, par2)
		Set ML1 = Doc.Select2("dfr5t456", ML.GetAppName, "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='SP'","",-1)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 3, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Müþteri")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen2, "Sipariþ No")
		if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
			res = pe1.Writebox(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen3, "Müþteri Sip. No")
		End if
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen4, "Sevk Boyu")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen5, "Teslim Tarihi")

		KutuUst = pe1.GetTop() - 1
		KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
		TMBasla = pe1.GetTop + satirAra
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to ML1.GetRowCount()
			satirPos = TMBasla + (i - 1) * satirYuksekligi
			CHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")
			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("wd3dfsdf", "STOK40T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i) & "'&TRNUM='" & ML1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
			SipPaketMiktari = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR(1)
			SipPaketIcerigi = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR(1)
			SipPaketIcerigiStr = trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(SipPaketIcerigi, 12,6))
			if trim(S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) <> "" Then
				SipPaketIcerigiStr = SipPaketIcerigiStr & " " & trim(S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE(1)) & " % " & trim(trim(S40T1.TOLERANS(1)))
			End if
			SipTeslimTarihi = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH(1)
			res = pe1.Write(satirPos, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1 - 2, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, ""))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, Gen2, trim(ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i)))
			if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
				res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen3, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", ML1.KAYNAKMPS(i), "")))
			End if
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen4, SipPaketMiktari & " x " & trim(Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(SipPaketIcerigi, 12,6)))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, Gen5, Doc.StrToNorDate(SipTeslimTarihi))

			Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
			res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
			res = pe1.Line(TMBasla - 1, SolMargin, 0, KutuSag - SolMargin)
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, SolMargin + Gen1, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
			if trim(OperasyonKodu) = "AMB" Then
				res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
			End if
			res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, pe1.GetLeft + Gen4, Alt - KutuUst, 0)
			res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, SolMargin, Alt - KutuUst, KutuSag - SolMargin)
		Next
	End if

	hmdSatirBasla = pe1.GetBottom() + satirYuksekligi
	MuhendislikSatirBasla = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
End Sub
Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, OpSatirNo)
	MamulKodu = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	YariMamulKodu = M10TI.R_YMAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = M10TI.R_KAYNAKTYPE(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = M10TI.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = M10E.MUSTERIKODU
	MPSNo = M10E.EVRAKNO
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
'	Tech.sort(">TSCODE")
	if Tech.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if (MuhendislikSatirBasla + (Tech.GetRecCount + 1) * satirYuksekligi) > AltMargin Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		MuhendislikSatirBasla = UstMargin
	End if

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(MuhendislikSatirBasla, solMargin, 5, 50, "Teknik Özellikler")
	MuhSatirBasla = MuhendislikSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi + 0.5
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Gen1 = 50
	res = pe1.Write(MuhSatirBasla, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Parametre")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Deðer")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	For i = 1 To Tech.GetRecCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			satirPozr = MuhSatirBasla + Satiryuksekligi
		Else
			satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom + 1
		End if
		if trim(Tech.TSCODE(i)) = "RESIM" Then
			DosyaAdi = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "IMAGES\" & trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i))
			sol = solMargin + Gen1 + 1
			res = pe1.NoPen()
			res = pe1.Picture(SatirPozr, sol, -2, sagMargin - sol - 1, DosyaAdi, 114)
		Else
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1))
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i)))
		End if

'		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1))
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, -2, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, Trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i)))
	Next
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = sagMargin
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(MuhSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
	HmdSatirBasla = pe1.GetBottom + 2
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla + satirYuksekligi - 0.4, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(MuhSatirBasla -1, solMargin + Gen1, KutuAlt - MuhSatirBasla + 1, 0)
End Sub
Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, satirNo, OpSatirNo)
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi

	HammaddeKodu = M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)
	HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,"")
	HammaddeMiktari = M10TH.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo)	
	HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,"")
	Gen1 = 35
	Gen2 = 65
	Gen3 = 20
	Gen4 = 10
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	if satirNo = 1 Then
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1, "Hammadde Kodu")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen2, "Hammadde Adý")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen3, "Miktar")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 2, 5,Gen4, "Birim")	
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		SatirPozr = hmdSatirBasla + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	End if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,SolMargin + 1, 5,Gen1, HammaddeKodu)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen2, HammaddeAdi)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Ondalik = M10TH.MaxSigDig("R_MIKTAR0")
	if Ondalik > 3 Then Ondalik = 3
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight, 5,Gen3, Doc.NumFormat(HammaddeMiktari, 12,Ondalik))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,pe1.GetRight + 2, 5,Gen4, HammaddeBirim)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	if satirNo = M10TH.GetRowCount() Then
		res = pe1.Box(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, KutuSag - solMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, solMargin + Gen1, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen2, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen3 + 2, KutuAlt - hmdSatirBasla + 1, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(hmdSatirBasla - 1 + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	End if
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.Write(Alt, SagMargin - 30,6,32," ")
End Sub
Sub Hazirlayan(pe1,SatirPozr)
	If (satirPozr + 30) > satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		satirPozr = UstMargin + 6
	End If
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, SolMargin, 6, (SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "HAZIRLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 5, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 6,(SagMargin-SolMargin) / 2, "ONAYLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 3, (SagMargin+SolMargin) / 2, 30,0)
	res = pe1.Line(satirPozr + 12, SolMargin, 0,SagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Box(satirPozr + 3, SolMargin, 30,SagMargin-SolMargin)
End Sub
Function GetAlternatifTezgah(UrunKodu, AlternatifKodu)
	GetAlternatifTezgah = ""
	if trim(AlternatifKodu) = "" Then Exit Function
	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & UrunKodu & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & AlternatifKodu & "'"
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("dwgt4gtgyy", "BOMU01T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() <= 1 Then Exit Function

	For i = 2 to B01T.GetRowCount
		GetAlternatifTezgah = GetAlternatifTezgah & ", " & trim(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
	Next
	GetAlternatifTezgah = Mid(GetAlternatifTezgah, 3)
End Function

Sub PrintForm()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
		if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "I" Then
			Set TECH10 = GetMuhendislik(BOMU01T, i)
			For j = 1 to TECH10.GetRowCount
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
				Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & satirNo
				if j = 1 Then
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Value = "Operasyon : " & BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD(i)
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Select
					Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

					satirNo = satirNo + 1
					Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & satirNo
				End if
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Value = "      " & Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", TECH10.TSCODE(j), -1)
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = TECH10.TSTEXT(j)
			Next
		End if
	Next
	Sh1.Columns(1).EntireColumn.AutoFit
	Sh1.Columns(2).EntireColumn.AutoFit
	Sh1.Cells(1, 1).Select

	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Function GetMuhendislik(B01T, SatirNo)
	MamulKodu = B01T.BOMREC_CODE(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = ""
	KaynakTipi = B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = B01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = ""
	MPSNo = ""
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)

	Set GetMuhendislik = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : PACK_LST1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK66
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirsayisi
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, OncekiKod, sol, PaketSayisi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, S66, S66T, CSipNo, CHKodu
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamMiktar, ToplamNet, ToplamBrut, ToplamKarton, Palettur
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid()
Public satirpozr, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi

Sub PrintForm()
	STOK66T.Safepack()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	Palettur = 0
	Palet = 0
	ToplamMiktar = 0
	ToplamNet = 0
	ToplamBrut = 0
	ToplamKarton = 0
	OncekiKod = ""
	
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	satiryuksekligi = 4.7
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 12.6
	sayfaSatirsayisi = 52
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Packing List", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	
	satirSonu = UstMargin + 172
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 30
	bos = ""
	Set S66 = Doc.Select2("A0B0C","STOK66T","","", -1)
	For i = 1 to S66.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S66.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = "" Then
			S66.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i) = S66.LOTNUMBER(i)
		Else
			S66.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "SF_IMIKTAR", S66.SIPARTNO(i), "")
		End if
	Next
	S66.GroupBy("SF_VRI_VR_R1+SF_VRI_NUM1+SF_VRI_NUM2")
	For i = 1 to S66.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S66.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) = trim(S66.LOTNUMBER(i)) Then
			S66.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	Call Alanlar()
	
	siraNo = 0
	
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	
	For k = 1 To S66.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, k)
	Next
	
	Call DipToplam(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	CHKodu = STOK66E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	CHAdi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,"")
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin,SolMargin ,8,SagMargin - SolMargin,"PACKING LIST")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - satiryuksekligi * 1.5,SolMargin,8,SagMargin - SolMargin,trim(CHAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK66E.TARIH(1))
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, SagMargin - 30, 5,10,"Date")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, SagMargin - 20, 5,20,": " & Tarih)
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	Call SBaslik(pe1, i)
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin + 2
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	
End Sub

Sub SBaslik(pe1, satirNo)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
	
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		Else
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		End if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.Writebox(satirpozr, Sol, satirYuksekligi * 2, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
		' res = pe1.Write(10, 10, 5, 30 , Baslik)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) <> "" Then
				xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	
	AlanDegeri = ""
	
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirsayisi or pe1.GetBottom >= altmargin Then
		
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		siraNo = 0
		
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
				
	End If
	
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + (siraNo) * satiryuksekligi
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 7)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		
		if Left(AlanAdi, 1) <> "_" Then
			AlanDegeri = S66.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
		Elseif Alanadi = "_AD" then
			StokKod = S66.KOD(satirNo)
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKod,"")
		Elseif Alanadi = "_ORDERSIP" then
			SipNo = S66.SIPNO(satirNo)
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK40E","CHSIPNO",SipNo,"")
		Elseif Alanadi = "_B_SF_MIKTAR" then
			if trim(S66.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo)) <> "" Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(S66.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo), Doc.CDbl(S66.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo))) & "x" & trim(S66.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo))
			Else
				AlanDegeri = "1x" & S66.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
			End if
		Elseif Alanadi = "_ITEM" then
			AlanDegeri = doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKod,CHKodu)
		End if
		
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		Else
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		
		res = pe1.Writebox(satirPozr, sol, satirYuksekligi, Grid(i,1), trim(AlanDegeri))
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8,6)
	Grid(0,0) = "SF_VRI_VR_R1"
	Grid(1,0) = "SF_VRI_NUM2"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_B_SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "NETAGIRLIK"
	Grid(6,0) = "BRUTAGIRLIK"
	Grid(7,0) = "_ITEM"
	Grid(8,0) = "_ORDERSIP"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 17
	Grid(1,1) = 17
	Grid(2,1) = 50
	Grid(3,1) = 17
	Grid(4,1) = 17
	Grid(5,1) = 14
	Grid(6,1) = 14
	Grid(7,1) = 22
	Grid(8,1) = 20
	' toplam 190
	
	Grid(0,2) = "PALLET SR. NO"
	Grid(1,2) = "DRUM SR. NO"
	Grid(2,2) = "MATERIAL"
	Grid(3,2) = "LENGHT OF REELS"
	Grid(4,2) = "TOTAL LENGTH"
	Grid(5,2) = "NET WEIGHT"
	Grid(6,2) = "GROSS WEIGHT"
	Grid(7,2) = "ITEM CODE"
	Grid(8,2) = "ORDER NO"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK66T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik
		End If
	Next
	
End Sub
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlari = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlari,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlari(i,2)
		AlanAdi = Alanlari(i,0)
		If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlari(i,0), Alanlari(i,3), Alanlari(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlari,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlari(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlari(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Sub AraToplamYaz(pe1, satirNo)
	Set S66T2 = Doc.Select2("wef43wfdfgd", "STOK66T", "KOD='" & S66.KOD(satirNo) & "'&NOTES='" & S66.NOTES(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	sayi = S66T2.GetRowCount()
	S66T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirsayisi or pe1.GetBottom >= altmargin Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		siraNo = 0
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	
	siraNo = siraNo + 2
	satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
	
	sol = solMargin + 1
	
	
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		if AlanAdi = "_TOTAL" Then
			AlanDegeri = "TOTAL"
		Elseif AlanAdi = "SERINO" Then
			AlanDegeri = Sayi
		Else
			AlanDegeri = S66T2.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,1)
		End if
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, sol, satirYuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
	
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
End Sub
Sub DipToplam(pe1)
	Set S66T2 = Doc.Select2("wef43wfdfgd", "STOK66T", "", "", -1)
	sayi = S66T2.GetRowCount()
	S66T2.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirsayisi or pe1.GetBottom >= altmargin Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		siraNo = 0
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	
	siraNo = siraNo + 2
	satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
	
	sol = solMargin + 1
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		'  res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		
		if AlanAdi = "SERINO" Then
			AlanDegeri = Sayi
		Elseif AlanAdi = "_B_SF_MIKTAR" Then
			AlanDegeri = S66T2.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,1)
		Else
			AlanDegeri = S66T2.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,1)
		End if
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + 1, sol + 1, satirYuksekligi-1, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Box(satirPozr, solmargin + 1, 6, sagMargin - solMargin+1)
End Sub

End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : PALET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20

Dim SSS20T1, S20TT, SS20, SSS20T, SSS20, SS40T, S20, MPS10, SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, ustMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen, Gen2

Sub PrintForm1(pe)
	Set pe1 = pe
	Call printform()
End Sub

Sub printform()
	
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	SayfaEni = 210
	ustMargin = 10
	solMargin = 15
	satirYuksekligi = 10
	satirYuksekligi2 = 6
	Gen = 40
	Gen2 = 130
	
	Set SS20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	if not IsObject(pe1) Then
		set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
		peYok = True
		res = pe1.Open(1,"PALET ETÝKETÝ" & trim(SS20E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")
	End if
	
	Call AmbalajSiparisBilgisi(pe1)

'	ret = BilgiYaz(pe1)
	

	if peYok Then
		res = pe1.show()
	Else
		pe1.PageBreak()
	End if
end sub

'*******************************************************************************************

Sub AmbalajSiparisBilgisi(pe1)

	Set SS20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	SS20T.safepack

	Set S20 = Doc.Select2("A0BC45xc","STOK20T", "", "",-1)
	for i = 1 to S20.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþ baðlantýlarý hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & S20.GetRecCount

		MPSNo = S20.MPSNO(i)
		M_ArtNo = S20.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		LotNo = S20.LOTNUMBER(i)

		Set MPS10 = Doc.Select1("A009Bc", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MPSNo)&"' & TRNUM='"&trim(M_ArtNo)&"'", "",-1)

		MusteriSipNo = MPS10.MUSTERISIPINO

		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("PAdBc", "STOK40E", "EVRAKNO='"&trim(MusteriSipNo)&"'", "",-1)
		
		MusteriSipNoORD = S40E.CHSIPNO
		SipTarihi = S40E.TARIH

		FaturaTip = S40E.MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK
		FTip = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("*CHHNT","KOD",FaturaTip,-1))
		
		S_ARTNO = MPS10.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
		S_ARTNO = TRIM(MusteriSipNo)+S_ARTNO
		
		Qstr2 = ""
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "ARTNO='"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + S_ARTNO
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + "'"
		Qstr2 = Qstr2 + ""
		
		Set SS40T = Doc.Select1("A90LFDSBC0xJKHSc", "STOK40T", Qstr2, "",-1)

		AmbTip = SS40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT
		AmbBoy = S20.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		S20.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(AmbBoy,10)
'		S20.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(MusteriSipNoORD),10)
		S20.X_ALC_SIPNO(i) = MusteriSipNoORD
'		S20.SF_VRI_RECETE(i) = MusteriSipNoORD
'		S20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i) = AmbTip
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = AmbTip
		S20.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = FTip

	Next
	Bos=""
	Set S20TT = Doc.Select2("A1LRcLP", S20.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1<>'"& Bos &"'", "",-1)
'	Set S20TT = Doc.Select2("A1LRcLP", S20.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_C='KANGAL'", "",-1)
'	Set S20TT = Doc.Select2("A2LRcLP", S20.GetAppName, "", "",-1)

'	Call SendTableToExcel(S20TT)

	satir = S20TT.GetRecCount()
	msgbox("Üretim fiþindeki kangallý mamül sayýsý" & " = "&satir)

	ret = BilgiYaz(pe1)

End Sub

'*******************************************************************************************

Function BilgiYaz(pe1)

'######
'	Set dd = doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
'######

	Sayfa = 0
	
		Ftipi= "XF"
	'	Ftipi= "FT"
	'	Set SS20 = Doc.Select2("A3B79C45k3xc", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R3='"&Ftipi&"'", "",-1)
		Set SS20 = Doc.Select2("A4xklpc", S20TT.GetAppName, "", "",-1)
	' Fatura tipine göre gruplandýrdýk

	if SS20.GetRecCount() > 1 then

		SS20.GroupBy("SF_VRI_VR_R1")
		'Seçili ürün listesinde Palet noya göre tekrar etmeyen kalemleri bulduk

		for j = 1 to SS20.GetRecCount()

			'********************************************
			t = 1
			Sayfa = sayfa + 1
			solmargin = 15
			'if Sayfa > 1 And Sayfa <= SS20.GetRecCount() then res = pe1.PageBreak()
			
			for t=1 to 2
			   res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 18)
			   res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			   res = pe1.write(ustMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen,"PALET NO")
			   res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi, Gen," : "&(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)))

	'Doc.d7Msgbox "1", "", MB_OK
				Set SSS20 = Doc.Select2("A5Zz11Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
				'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik

	'Doc.d7Msgbox "2", "", MB_OK
				SSS20.GroupBy("SF_VRI_NUM1")
	'Doc.d7Msgbox "21", "", MB_OK
				'Seçili ürün listesinde sevk boyuna göre tekrar etmeyen boylarý bulduk

				for k = 1 to SSS20.GetRecCount()

					skod = SSS20.KOD(k)
					sipno = SSS20.X_ALC_SIPNO(k)
					Atip = SSS20.SF_VRI_VR_C(k)
					ambBoy = SSS20.SF_VRI_NUM1(k)
					birim = SSS20.SF_SF_UNIT(k)
					stokkod = SSS20.KOD(k)
					MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
					Paletno = SSS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(k)

				'Doc.d7Msgbox "3", "", MB_OK
						Set SSS20T = Doc.Select2("A6Yaa1Y0", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_NUM1='"& ambBoy & "' & KOD='"& skod & "' & SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Paletno & "'", "",-1)
						'Bir Mamümülün sevk boyunun o sipariþteki listesini bulduk

						PaketSayisi = SSS20T.GetRecCount()
						ambBoy = Doc.Cdbl(trim(SSS20.SF_VRI_NUM1(k)))
						partimik = PaketSayisi * ambBoy
						res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
						res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi+2, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen2, MamulAdi)
						res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
						res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Gen2-30,(paketsayisi &"x"&Trim(ambBoy) &"="&partimik & " " & birim))
						res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

						if SSS20T.GetRecCount() = 1 then
							Call Etiket(pe1,SSS20,k)
						End if

					'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi+25, solMargin, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, SSS20.SF_VRI_NUM1(k))
					'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, SSS20.SF_VRI_VR_C(k))

				Next
				
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)

				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi*2, solMargin, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, "P/O NO")
				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, " : " & trim(SS20.X_ALC_SIPNO(j)))
				
				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi2, solMargin, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, "PRODUCTION")
				res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, " : " & tariha(SS20E.TARIH))
			'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi2, solMargin, satirYuksekligi2, Gen, SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R3(j))

				res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			'	solmargin = solmargin*3+Gen2

			next
			if Sayfa => 1 And Sayfa < SS20.GetRecCount() then res = pe1.PageBreak()


		Next

	else
		msgbox("Ýlgili Üretim Fiþinde Kangallý Ýhracat Ürünü yer almamaktadýr!...")
	End if
end Function

'*******************************************************************************************

Sub Etiket(pe1, SSS20,k)

'	Set S20TS = Doc.Select2("A5Ac", S20TT.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & Trim(SS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j)) & "'", "",-1)
'	'ilgili Paletnolu urunleri listeledik

	skod = SSS20.KOD(k)
	sipno = SSS20.X_ALC_SIPNO(k)
	Atip = SSS20.SF_VRI_VR_C(k)
	ambBoy = SSS20.SF_VRI_NUM1(k)
	birim = SSS20.SF_SF_UNIT(k)
	stokkod = SSS20.KOD(k)
	MamulAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", stokkod, "")
	Paletno = SSS20.SF_VRI_VR_R1(k)
	Lotno = SSS20.MPSNO(k)
	Serino = SSS20.SERINO(k)

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dee45t6777", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SSS20.SERINO), 30) & "'", "", -1)

	Olusturmatarihi = D7LB.CREA_TARIH

'	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
'	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 1,Sol,5,-2,"\\Dnmsrv\dnmerp\d7k\IMAGES\logo.jpg", 114)
'	res = pe1.NoPen()

'	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+satirYuksekligi, solMargin, satirYuksekligi

	EtiketBoyu = 40
	EtiketEni = 56
	ust = pe1.GetTop - 7
	sol = solmargin + Gen2

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(ust, sol, 11, EtiketEni)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(ust + 0.5, sol + 1, 12, EtiketEni - 2, trim(MamulAdi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	res = pe1.Write(ust + 11, sol+1, 4, 25, Trim(Lotno))
	res = pe1.Write(ust + 15, sol+1, 4, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Olusturmatarihi))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 11, (sol+EtiketEni) - 26, 8, 25, trim(ambBoy) & " " & trim(birim))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow",8)
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 20, sol + 5, 13, EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", trim(Serino), "")	
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

End Sub

'************************************************************************************************************

Function MyNorDate(Tarih)
	MyNorDate = Right(Tarih, 2) & "." & Mid(Tarih, 6, 2) & "." & Left(Tarih, 4)
End Function

'************************************************************************************************************

Function tariha(date)
	ay = mid(date,6,2)
	yil = left(date,4)
	
	aylar = Array("JANUARY","FEBRUARY","MARCH","APRIL","MAY","JUNE","JULY","AUGUST","SEPTEMBER","OCTOBER","NOVEMBER","DECEMBER")
	ay = ay-1
	tariha = aylar(ay) & " " & yil
End Function

Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
