Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, S30T
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	
	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 10

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Sevk Emri" & trim(STOK30E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	STOK30T.SafePack()
	Set S30T = Doc.Select2("sese222", "STOK30T", "", "", -1)
	S30T.GroupBy("OALLOC_SIPARTNO")
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 to S30T.GetRowCount()
		Call PrintFormSatir(pe1, i)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub Print_Form_Sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin, solMargin, 15, 40, logo, 114)
	Gen10 = 25
	Gen11 = 60
	Gen20 = 20

	bBasla = UstMargin + 5
	SatirAra = 6
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin, 8, sagMargin - solmargin,"Sevk Emri")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(BBasla, sagMargin - 40, 6, 40, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun) & " " & Doc.Saat(5))

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra + 8, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Sevk Emri No")
	Ust = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(STOK30E.EVRAKNO))
	Blok2 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin, 6, Gen10, "Fiili Sevk")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6, Gen11, ": " & Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK30E.EVRAKNO))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, SolMargin, 6,Gen10, "Müþteri Tem.")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "AD_SOYAD", Doc.Getd7UserName, ""))

	Set S40E = Doc.Select1("swr43wgg55", "STOK40E", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK30T.OALLOC_SIPNO(1) & "'", "", -1)
	SevkAdresi = trim(S40E.SEVKADRESI_1) & " " & trim(S40E.SEVKADRESI_2) & " " & trim(S40E.SEVKADRESI_2)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin, 6, Gen10, "Sevk Adresi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,10, Gen11, ": " & trim(SevkAdresi))

	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("swr43wgg55x", "CARI00", "KOD='" & S40E.MUSTERIKODU & "'", "", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & C00.AD)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Tel/Faks")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & trim(C00.TELEFONNO_1) & " / " & trim(C00.FAXNO))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Adres")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Adres = trim(trim(C00.ADRES_1) & " " & trim(C00.ADRES_2) & " " & trim(C00.ADRES_3))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 12, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Adres)
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK30
Hangi Tablo için : STOK30
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Emri
Kod : 
Dim sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, S30T, ToplamMiktar, solMiktar, GenMiktar
Dim siraNo, satirBaslangic,SolMargin, SagMargin, Ustmargin, AltMargin
Dim satirYuksekligi, xCizgi, Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	If Doc.Save_Voucher() <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	STOK30T.SafePack()
	Set S30T = Doc.Select2("dwf4rff", "STOK30T", "", "", -1)
	S30T.Sort("OALLOC_SIPARTNO")

	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	siraNo = 0
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5
	ToplamMiktar = 0

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	res = pe1.Open(0, VPEAdi, SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10
	satirSonu = AltMargin
	Call Alanlar()

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To S30T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMiktar - 15, 5, 15, "Toplam")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMiktar, 5, genMiktar - 1, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMiktar, 15, MiktarOnd))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	ToplamMiktar = 0
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))

	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin, solMargin, 15, 40, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	Blok2 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,10, Gen11, ": " & trim(SevkAdresi))

	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("swr43wgg55x", "CARI00", "KOD='" & S40E.MUSTERIKODU & "'", "", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & C00.AD)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Tel/Faks")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & trim(C00.TELEFONNO_1) & " / " & trim(C00.FAXNO))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok2, 6,Gen20, "Adres")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Adres = trim(trim(C00.ADRES_1) & " " & trim(C00.ADRES_2) & " " & trim(C00.ADRES_3))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 12, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Adres)

'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 11
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			If Grid(i,0) = "SF_MIKTAR" Then
				solMiktar = sol
				GenMiktar = SutunGenisligi
			End if
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
			ReDim Preserve xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi) + 1)		
		End If
	Next

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	Dim satirpozr
	if satirNo > 1 Then
		if S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) <> S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo-1) Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMiktar - 15, 5, 15, "Toplam")
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMiktar, 5, genMiktar - 1, Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMiktar, 15, MiktarOnd))
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			ToplamMiktar = 0
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		End if
	End if
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		ReDim xCizgi(0)
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	ToplamMiktar = ToplamMiktar + S30T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		Degisken = S30T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
	If EnAlt = 0 Or EnAlt > SayfaBoyu Then EnAlt = AltMargin 
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - 10
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
	res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, EnAlt - KutuUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)-2
	  LineX = xCizgi(i)
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	  res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, EnAlt - KutuUst, 0)
	Next
'dikey çizgiler
End Sub
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, SagMargin - 45,5,12,"Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2, pe1.GetRight,5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight,":  " & i & "  /  " & pe1.GetPageCount())
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, SagMargin - 45,5,12,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 8, pe1.GetRight,5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight,":  " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun()) & " " & Doc.Saat(5))
	Next
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,6)
	Grid(0,0) = "OALLOC_SIPNO"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(4,0) = "SERINO"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"

	Grid(0,1) = 20		
	Grid(1,1) = 26		
	Grid(2,1) = 70		
	Grid(3,1) = 20		
	Grid(4,1) = 20		
	Grid(5,1) = 20		
	Grid(6,1) = 10		

	Grid(0,2) = "Sipariþ No"				
	Grid(1,2) = "Stok Kodu"				
	Grid(2,2) = "Stok Adý"				
	Grid(3,2) = "Lot No"				
	Grid(4,2) = "Seri No"				
	Grid(5,2) = "Miktar"				
	Grid(6,2) = "Birim"				

	Grid(0,4) = "C"	
	Grid(1,4) = "C"	
	Grid(2,4) = "C"	
	Grid(3,4) = "C"	
	Grid(4,4) = "C"	
	Grid(5,4) = "N"	
	Grid(6,4) = "C"	

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = S30T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			if Grid(i,0) = "SF_MIKTAR" Then MiktarOnd = Ondalik
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK40-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Order Confirmation
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sayfaSatirSayisi, sayfaNoUst, Blok2, Gen11, SonSayfa, solTutar, genTutar
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi, solFiyat, GenFiyat
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic
Public xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 9
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 22
	SolMargin = 18
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 30
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Order Confirmation-" & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	SonSayfa = False
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	STOK40T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 To STOK40T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	SonSayfa = True
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	Call LogoYaz(pe1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

	SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH)
	CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU
	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("e3434", "CARI00", "KOD='" & STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU & "'", "", -1)
	CHAdi = C00.AD
	Adres1 = C00.ADRES_1
	Adres2 = C00.ADRES_2
	Adres3 = C00.ADRES_3
	Tel = C00.TELEFONNO_1
	Faks = C00.FAXNO
	email = C00.EMAIL
	ilgili = STOK40E.SIPIALAN

	Gen1 = 26
	Gen2 = 76
	Aralik = 6

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+30, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Customer :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 1, 5, 100, CHAdi)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Order No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen2,  ": " & trim(STOK40E.CHSIPNO))

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Enquiry No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen2,  ": " & STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Payment")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,  ": " & trim(STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1) & " " & trim(STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2))
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Delivery")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,  ": " & trim(STOK40E.NAKLIYEKOSULLARI))

'	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Destination")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,  ": " & trim(Destination))
	BaslikBasla = pe1.GetBottom + 2


	Gen11 = 20
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+30, SolMargin + 125, 6,Gen11, "Our Ref.")
	Blok2 = pe1.GetLeft()
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO))

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6,Gen11, "Date")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK40E.TARIH))
	sayfaNoUst = pe1.GetTop + Aralik
	
	satirBaslangic = BaslikBasla + 11
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) = "" Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			End if
		End if
		res = pe1.write(BaslikBasla, Sol, 11, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) <> "" Then
				xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If		
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "OR_TUTAR" Then
			solTutar = sol
			genTutar = SutunGenisligi
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "OR_FIYAT" Then
			solFiyat = sol
			genFiyat = SutunGenisligi
		End if
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
'				Degisken = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo), STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU)
				Degisken = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", STOK40T.KOD(satirNo), "EN_AD", 0)
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SHPDATE" Then
				Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("D7DATE")
				d7Date.SetDate STOK40T.REF_TARIH_1(satirNo)
				Degisken = d7Date.GetWOY_ISO8601()
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_PACKING" Then
				if trim(STOK40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT(satirNo)) = "KANGAL" Then
					Degisken = "C" & STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR(satirNo)
				Elseif trim(STOK40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT(satirNo)) = "MAKARA" Then
					Degisken = "D" & STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR(satirNo)
				Else
					Degisken = STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR(satirNo)
				End if
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - 12
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, KutuAlt - KutuUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To uBound(xCizgi)
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, KutuAlt - KutuUst, 0)
	Next


	if Not SonSayfa Then Exit Sub
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt, xCizgi(2), satirYuksekligi, xCizgi(4) - xCizgi(2))
	res = pe1.Line(KutuAlt, xCizgi(3), satirYuksekligi, 0)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(KutuAlt + 1, solFiyat, 5, GenFiyat, "Grand Total")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solTutar, 5, GenTutar - 1.5, Doc.NumFormat(STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM, 18,2))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	satirPoz = pe1.GetBottom +  1 - satirYuksekligi

	if trim(STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1) <> "" Then
		satirPoz = satirPoz + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirPoz, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
	End if
	if trim(STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2) <> "" Then
		satirPoz = satirPoz + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirPoz, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
	End if
	if trim(STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3) <> "" Then
		satirPoz = satirPoz + satirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirPoz, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)
	End if

	Aciklama = STOK40NT_B.ACIKLAMA
	satirPoz = satirPoz
	if trim(Aciklama) <> "" Then
		AciklamaArr = Split(Aciklama, vbcrlf)
	
		For i = 0 to uBound(AciklamaArr)
			satirPoz = satirPoz + satirYuksekligi
			Call ControlWrite(pe1, satirPoz, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, rtrim(AciklamaArr(i)))
		Next
	End if
End Sub
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,6)
	Grid(0,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(1,0) = "_PACKING"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1"
	Grid(6,0) = "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2"

	Grid(0,1) = 65
	Grid(1,1) = 15
	Grid(2,1) = 17
	Grid(3,1) = 21
	Grid(4,1) = 25
	Grid(5,1) = 18.5
	Grid(6,1) = 23

	Grid(0,2) = "Product"
	Grid(1,2) = "Packing"
	Grid(2,2) = "Qty" & vbcrlf & "(m)"
	Grid(3,2) = "Price" & vbcrlf & "(" & trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1)) & "/m)"
	Grid(4,2) = "Total" & vbcrlf & "(" & trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1)) & ")"
	Grid(5,2) = "Shipment Date"
	Grid(6,2) = "LME Avg."

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			if Grid(i,0) = "OR_TUTAR" Then
				Grid(i,6) = 2		
			Else
				Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			End if
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub LogoYaz(pe1)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If trim(logo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, SolMargin + 0.5, 17,42,logo, 114)
	End If
       
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 5, pe1.GetRight + 1,8, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2,"SURTEL KABLO SANAYÝÝ A.Þ.")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 18)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 20, solMargin,8, SagMargin - solMargin,"ORDER CONFIRMATION")
End Sub
Sub AltBilgiYaz(pe1)
	res = pe1.Nopen()
'	res = pe1.Picture(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 10, 120, stdLogo, 114)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Century Gothic", 9)
	Adres = "Sefaköy Fevzi Çakmak Caddesi No.9 34295 Sefaköy - ÝSTANBUL - TURKEY"
	TelFaks = "Tel.: +90 212 411 16 00 (pbx)  Faks: +90 212 624 25 78 - 624 21 58"
	webMail = "www.surtel.com.tr  e-mail : info@surtel.com.tr"

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, Adres)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, TelFaks)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, webMail)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	For i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, sagMargin - 40, 5, 10, "Page")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, i & " of " & pe1.GetPageCount())

		Call AltBilgiYaz(pe1)
	Next
End Sub
Sub ControlWrite(pe1, Ust, sol, Yukseklik, En, Yazi)
	if Ust >= (AltMargin - satirYuksekligi) Then
		res = pe1.PageBreak
		Call LogoYaz(pe1)
		Ust = UstMargin + 30
	End if
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, sol, Yukseklik, En, Yazi)
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK40-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Yurt Ýçi Satýþ Sipariþi - Dövizli
Kod : 
Dim sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, solTutar, GenTutar
Dim UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Dim siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla, xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Call BakirAgirligiveToplamBrutHesapla()
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 8
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 48
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	siraNo = 0        
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To STOK40T.GetRecCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin - 6,SolMargin,8,35,"SATIÞ SÝPARÝÞÝ")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin -6,SagMargin - 150,8,150,trim(FirmaAdi))
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))

		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 4
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHKodu)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra
		If trim(cHadi2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi2)
		End If
		If trim(Adres1) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres1)
		End If
		If trim(Adres2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres2)
		End If

		If trim(Adres3) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres3)
		End If

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi Dairesi / No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 42, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 45, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,30,"No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Satýcý")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Satici)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþi Veren")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Yetkili)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)


' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then			
				if Grid(i+1, 2) = "" Then
					SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
				End if
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 11, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat0(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				Else
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					Elseif grid(i,0) = "_TOLERANS" Then
						Degisken = STOK40T.TOLERANSTYPE(satirNo) & " % " & Doc.NumFormat0(STOK40T.TOLERANS(satirNo), 15,0)
					End If
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)


'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 12, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12, SagMargin - SolMargin)


'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi,1) - 2
		If Grid(i+1, 2) <> "" Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 12, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12, SagMargin - SolMargin)


'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi,1) - 2
		If Grid(i+1, 2) <> "" Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)


	Blok2 = SolMargin + 35
	Genislik = xCizgi(4) - Blok2 - 2		
	satirpozr = SatirBitis + 2

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Ödeme")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 32, 5,3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Blok2, 5,Genislik,trim(Odeme1))

	If trim(odeme2)<> "" Then
		satirpozr = SatirPozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Blok2, 5,Genislik,trim(Odeme2))
	End If		

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Notlar")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 32, 5,3,":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not1)

	If trim(Not2)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not2)
	End If			

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Sevk Adresi")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 32, 5,3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi1)

	If trim(sevkAdresi2)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi2)
	End If
	If trim(sevkAdresi3)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi3)
	End If
		
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom() + 2

	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	
	sss = SatirYuksekligi

	SatirYuksekligi = 6

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2, solTutar - 30,6,30,"TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,40,"ÝSKONTO %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,30,"NET TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar - 30,6,30,"GENEL TOPLAM")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2, solTutar,6, GenTutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,solTutar,6,GenTutar, IskontoToplami)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi - 1, solTutar,0, GenTutar)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar,6,GenTutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,12,GenTutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi - 1,solTutar,0,GenTutar)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,0,GenTutar)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,6,GenTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
'ppp
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(11,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "NOTES"
	Grid(3,0) = "PMIKTAR_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_IMIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "_TOLERANS"
	Grid(8,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(9,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(10,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	Grid(11,0) = "RTESTARIH"

	Grid(0,1) = 30
	Grid(1,1) = 69
	Grid(2,1) = 46
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 13
	Grid(5,1) = 19
	Grid(6,1) = 10
	Grid(7,1) = 12
	Grid(8,1) = 16
	Grid(9,1) = 10
	Grid(10,1) = 23
	Grid(11,1) = 19

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Kodu"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklama"
	Grid(2,2) = "Notlar"
	Grid(3,2) = "Sevk Þekli"
	Grid(4,2) = "Sevk Boyu"
	Grid(5,2) = "Sipariþ Miktarý"
	Grid(6,2) = ""
	Grid(7,2) = "Tol."
	Grid(8,2) = "Net Satýþ Fiyatý"
	Grid(9,2) = ""
	Grid(10,2) = "Tutar"
	Grid(11,2) = "Teslim Tarihi"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "D"

	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" or grid(i,0) = "OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		if InStr(1, Grid(i,0), "_TUTAR") > 0 Then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub BakirAgirligiveToplamBrutHesapla()
End Sub

Makro Kodu : STOK40-3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Yurt Ýçi Satýþ Sipariþi - TL
Kod : 
Dim sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, solTutar, GenTutar
Dim UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Dim siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirAra, SatirBitis, bBasla, xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Call BakirAgirligiveToplamBrutHesapla()
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15 
	SolMargin = 8
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 48
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 20 * satirYuksekligi
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satis Siparisi - " & trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)),  SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	Call Alanlar()
	siraNo = 0        
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To STOK40T.GetRecCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
		FirmaAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.WriteBox(UstMargin - 6,SolMargin,8,35,"SATIÞ SÝPARÝÞÝ")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin -6,SagMargin - 150,8,150,trim(FirmaAdi))
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))

		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		CHAdi2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Yetkili = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
		GK1 = STOK40E.GK_1(1)		
		GK2 = STOK40E.GK_2(1)		
		GK3 = STOK40E.GK_3(1)		
		GK1 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",GK1,-1)
		GK2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK2","AD",GK2,-1)
		GK3 = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK3","AD",GK3,-1)

		MusteriSipNo = STOK40E.CHSIPNO(1)
		Satici = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)


		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		bBasla = UstMargin + 4
		SatirAra = 5.2
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHKodu)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + satirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi)
		satirPozr = BBasla + satirAra
		If trim(cHadi2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,cHadi2)
		End If
		If trim(Adres1) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres1)
		End If
		If trim(Adres2) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres2)
		End If

		If trim(Adres3) <> "" Then
			satirPozr = satirPozr + satirAra
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, SolMargin + 2, 6,100,Adres3)
		End If

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"Vergi Dairesi / No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 42, 6,2,": ")
		res = pe1.Write(satirPozr + SatirAra, SolMargin + 45, 6,100,trim(VergiDairesi) & " / " & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 185, 6,30,"No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Satýcý")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Satici)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sipariþi Veren")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,70,": " & Yetkili)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)


' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then			
				if Grid(i+1, 2) = "" Then
					SutunGenisligi = SutunGenisligi + Grid(i+1,1)
				End if
			End If
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 11, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				If Left(DegiskenAdi,1) <> "_" Then
					Degisken = STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
					res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
					If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat0(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
					ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
						Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
					End If
				Else
					If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" Then
						res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
						Miktar = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(SatirNo)
						IndTutar = STOK40T.TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(SatirNo)
						Degisken = Doc.Div(IndTutar, Miktar)
						Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken,18,2)
					Elseif grid(i,0) = "_TOLERANS" Then
						Degisken = STOK40T.TOLERANSTYPE(satirNo) & " % " & Doc.NumFormat0(STOK40T.TOLERANS(satirNo), 15,0)
					Elseif grid(i,0) = "_PRICEUNIT" Then
						Degisken = Doc.DefaultPB
					End If
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
				SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_AraSayfa(pe1)


'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 12, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12, SagMargin - SolMargin)


'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi,1) - 2
		If Grid(i+1, 2) <> "" Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dýþ çerçeve
	SatirBitis = SatirSonu
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.8)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 12, SolMargin, satirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12, SagMargin - SolMargin)


'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - satirBaslangic + 12
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi,1) - 2
		If Grid(i+1, 2) <> "" Then
			LineX = xCizgi(i)		
			res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
		End If
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic - 2, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)

	Odeme1 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1(1)
	Odeme2 = STOK40E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2(1)
	Not1 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Not2 = STOK40E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	sevkAdresi1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
	sevkAdresi2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
	sevkAdresi3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)


	Blok2 = SolMargin + 35
	Genislik = xCizgi(4) - Blok2 - 2		
	satirpozr = SatirBitis + 2

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Ödeme")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 32, 5,3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Blok2, 5,Genislik,trim(Odeme1))

	If trim(odeme2)<> "" Then
		satirpozr = SatirPozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Blok2, 5,Genislik,trim(Odeme2))
	End If		

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Notlar")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 32, 5,3,":")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not1)

	If trim(Not2)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, Not2)
	End If			

	satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Solmargin + 2, 5,30,"Sevk Adresi")
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr ,Solmargin + 32, 5,3,": " )
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi1)

	If trim(sevkAdresi2)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi2)
	End If
	If trim(sevkAdresi3)<> "" Then  
		satirpozr = satirpozr + SatirYuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Blok2,6,Genislik, sevkAdresi3)
	End If
		
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom() + 2

	Ondalik = 2
	ToplamTutar = STOK40E.TL_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	IskontoOrani = STOK40E.DO_ISKONTOX_ORANI(1)
	IskontoToplami=STOK40E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK40E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVOrani = STOK40E.DO_KDV_ORANI(1)

	ToplamTutar = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoOrani = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoOrani,18,Ondalik)
	IskontoToplami = Doc.NumFormat(IskontoToplami,18,Ondalik)
	NetTutar = Doc.NumFormat(NetTutar,18,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(KDVOrani,18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari,18,Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar,18,Ondalik)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	
	sss = SatirYuksekligi

	SatirYuksekligi = 6

	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2, solTutar - 30,6,30,"TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,40,"ÝSKONTO %  " & IskontoOrani)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,30,"NET TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,pe1.GetLeft,6,40,"KDV %  " & KDVOrani)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar - 30,6,30,"GENEL TOPLAM")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2, solTutar,6, GenTutar, ToplamTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + satirYuksekligi,solTutar,6,GenTutar, IskontoToplami)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 2 * satirYuksekligi - 1, solTutar,0, GenTutar)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solTutar,6,GenTutar, NetTutar)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 2 + 3 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,12,GenTutar, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi - 1,solTutar,0,GenTutar)
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis + 2 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,0,GenTutar)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(SatirBitis + 3.5 + 4 * satirYuksekligi,solTutar,6,GenTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub
'ppp
Sub SayfaSayisi(pe1)
	If pe1.GetPageCount > 1 Then
		For i = 1 To pe1.GetPageCount
			res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 185, 6,26,"Sayfa")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4 * SatirAra, SolMargin + 215, 6,50,": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount)
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(11,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "NOTES"
	Grid(3,0) = "PMIKTAR_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_IMIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "_TOLERANS"
	Grid(8,0) = "_IFIYAT"
	Grid(9,0) = "_PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(10,0) = "TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR"
	Grid(11,0) = "RTESTARIH"

	Grid(0,1) = 30
	Grid(1,1) = 69
	Grid(2,1) = 46
	Grid(3,1) = 15
	Grid(4,1) = 13
	Grid(5,1) = 19
	Grid(6,1) = 10
	Grid(7,1) = 12
	Grid(8,1) = 16
	Grid(9,1) = 10
	Grid(10,1) = 23
	Grid(11,1) = 19

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Kodu"
	Grid(1,2) = "Açýklama"
	Grid(2,2) = "Notlar"
	Grid(3,2) = "Sevk Þekli"
	Grid(4,2) = "Sevk Boyu"
	Grid(5,2) = "Sipariþ Miktarý"
	Grid(6,2) = ""
	Grid(7,2) = "Tol."
	Grid(8,2) = "Net Satýþ Fiyatý"
	Grid(9,2) = ""
	Grid(10,2) = "Tutar"
	Grid(11,2) = "Teslim Tarihi"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "D"

	PB = trim(STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	Sol = SolMargin
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
		If grid(i,0) = "_IFIYAT" or grid(i,0) = "TL_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR" Then grid(i,6) = 2
		SutunGenisligi = grid(i,1)
		if InStr(1, Grid(i,0), "_TUTAR") > 0 Then
			solTutar = sol
			GenTutar = SutunGenisligi
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Sub BakirAgirligiveToplamBrutHesapla()
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK46
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Sipariþ Fomu
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sayfaSatirSayisi, sayfaNoUst, Blok2, Gen21, SonSayfa
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic
Public xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 9
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 32
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 21
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Siparisi-" & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	SonSayfa = False
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	SonSayfa = True
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If trim(logo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, SolMargin + 0.5, 17,42,logo, 114)
	End If
       
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 5, pe1.GetRight + 1,8, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2,"SURTEL KABLO SANAYÝÝ A.Þ.")

	SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH)
	CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("e3434", "CARI00", "KOD='" & STOK46E.CH_KODU & "'", "", -1)
	CHAdi = C00.AD
	Adres1 = C00.ADRES_1
	Adres2 = C00.ADRES_2
	Adres3 = C00.ADRES_3
	Tel = C00.TELEFONNO_1
	Faks = C00.FAXNO
	email = C00.EMAIL
	ilgili = STOK46E.SIPIALAN

	Gen1 = 34
	Gen2 = 76
	Aralik = 6
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+30, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Sipariþ No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen2,  ": " & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))
	sayfaNoUst = pe1.GetTop()
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen2,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH))

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tedarikçi Kodu")
	TedUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen2,": " & STOK46E.CH_KODU)
	Blok2 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tedarikçi Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & CHAdi)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Sip. Veren")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & STOK46E.SIPIVEREN)

	Gen21 = 18
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sayfaNoUst + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Talep No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & trim(STOK46E.NAKLIYECIFIRMA))

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Telefon")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Tel)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Faks")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Faks)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "e-mail")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & email)

	if trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) = "" Then
		Odeme = "Peþin"
	Else
		if trim(STOK46E.FIYSEKLI) = "SP" Then
			Odeme = "Sipariþ tarihinden itibaren "
		Else
			Odeme = "Fatura tarihinden itibaren "
		End if
		Odeme = Odeme & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) & " gün"
	End if
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Ödeme")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Odeme)
		
	Str1 = "Zamanýnda teslim etmek kaydýyla, aþaðýda dökümü yapýlan malzemelerin sipariþe alýnmasýný rica ederiz" & vbcrlf & _
			"Bozuk olan mallarýn iadesi yapýlacaktýr" & vbcrlf & "Lütfen faks ile teyit ediniz."

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 2, solMargin, -2, sagMargin - solMargin, Str1)
	
	BaslikBasla = pe1.GetBottom + 4
	satirBaslangic = BaslikBasla + 6
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) = "" Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			End if
		End if
		res = pe1.write(BaslikBasla, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) <> "" Then
				xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If		
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
				Degisken = satirNo
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - 7
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, KutuAlt - KutuUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	if SonSayfa Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom +  1, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	End if
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To uBound(xCizgi)
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, KutuAlt - KutuUst, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 35, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Saygýlarýmýzla")

	KutuGen = 122
	KutuSol = (sayfaEni - KutuGen) / 2
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop + 6, KutuSol, 26.5, KutuGen)
	KutuUst = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + 10, KutuSol, 0, KutuGen)
	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, KutuSol + KutuGen/2, 10, 0)
	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Fikret TUNÇMAN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Genel Md. Yrd.")

	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, pe1.GetRight, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Ani GÖÇER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Satýnalma Müd.")

	res = pe1.Nopen()
'	res = pe1.Picture(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 10, 120, stdLogo, 114)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Century Gothic", 9)
	Adres = "Sefaköy Fevzi Çakmak Caddesi No.9 34630 Sefaköy - ÝSTANBUL - TURKEY"
	TelFaks = "Tel.: +90 212 624 32 46 (pbx)  Faks: +90 212 624 25 78 - 624 21 58"
	webMail = "www.surtel.com.tr  e-mail : info@surtel.com.tr"

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 0, 120)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, Adres)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, TelFaks)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, webMail)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,6)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid	(7,0) = "NOTE0"

	Grid(0,1) = 8
	Grid(1,1) = 56
	Grid(2,1) = 16
	Grid(3,1) = 7
	Grid(4,1) = 23
	Grid(5,1) = 7
	Grid(6,1) = 21
	Grid(7,1) = 53

	Grid(0,2) = "SN"
	Grid(1,2) = "Mamül Adý"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktarý"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "Birim Fiyatý"
	Grid(5,2) = ""
	Grid(6,2) = "Termin T."
	Grid(7,2) = "Açýklama"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "D"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	For i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaNoUst, Blok2, 5, Gen21, "Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount())
	Next
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK46-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Bakýr Satýnalma Sipariþ Fomu
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sayfaSatirSayisi, sayfaNoUst, Blok2, Gen21, SonSayfa
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic
Public xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 9
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 32
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 21
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Siparisi-" & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	SonSayfa = False
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	SonSayfa = True
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If trim(logo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, SolMargin + 0.5, 17,42,logo, 114)
	End If
       
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

			End if
		End if
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If		
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
				Degisken = satirNo
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_FIYAT" Then
				Degisken = "LME+"
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - 11
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.box(KutuUst, SolMargin, KutuAlt - KutuUst, SagMargin - SolMargin)
	if SonSayfa Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom +  1, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, "Lütfen faturalarýnýzda sipariþ no ve satýr no belirtiniz")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop +  satirYuksekligi, solMargin+1, 5, sagMargin - solMargin-1, STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4)
	End if
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To uBound(xCizgi)
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, LineX, KutuAlt - KutuUst, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 35, solMargin, 5, sagMargin - solMargin, "Saygýlarýmýzla")

	KutuGen = 122
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	KutuSol = (sayfaEni - KutuGen) / 2
	res = pe1.Box(AltMargin - 29, KutuSol, 26.5, KutuGen)
	KutuUst = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + 10, KutuSol, 0, KutuGen)
'	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, KutuSol + KutuGen/2, 10, 0)
	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Fikret ÇOBANOÐLU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Genel Müdür")

	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, pe1.GetRight, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Fikret TUNÇMAN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Genel Md. Yrd.")

	res = pe1.Nopen()
'	res = pe1.Picture(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 10, 120, stdLogo, 114)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Century Gothic", 9)
	Adres = "Sefaköy Fevzi Çakmak Caddesi No.9 34630 Sefaköy - ÝSTANBUL - TURKEY"
	TelFaks = "Tel.: +90 212 624 32 46 (pbx)  Faks: +90 212 624 25 78 - 624 21 58"
	webMail = "www.surtel.com.tr  e-mail : info@surtel.com.tr"

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 0, 120)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, Adres)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, TelFaks)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, webMail)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,6)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "_FIYAT"
	Grid(4,0) = "NOTE0"
	Grid(5,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"

	Grid(0,1) = 8
	Grid(1,1) = 56
	Grid(2,1) = 23
	Grid(3,1) = 30
	Grid(4,1) = 53
	Grid(5,1) = 21

	Grid(0,2) = "SN"
	Grid(1,2) = "Mamül Adý"
	Grid(2,2) = "Miktarý " & vbcrlf & "(kg)"
	Grid(3,2) = "Birim Fiyatý" & vbcrlf & "USD/TON"
	Grid(4,2) = "LME Ortalamasý"
	Grid(5,2) = "Termin T."

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "D"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	For i = 1 to pe1.GetPageCount()
		res = pe1.GotoPage(i)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(sayfaNoUst, Blok2, 5, Gen21, "Sayfa")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & i & " / " & pe1.GetPageCount())
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK46-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Bakýr Satýnalma Sipariþ Fomu Vkl
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sayfaSatirSayisi, sayfaNoUst, Blok2, Gen21, SonSayfa
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic
Public xCizgi, Grid

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	siraNo = 0
	UstMargin = 9
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 32
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 21
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Siparisi-" & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO), SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	SonSayfa = False
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	SonSayfa = True
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	Call SayfaNoYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	If trim(logo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 0.5, SolMargin + 0.5, 17,42,logo, 114)
	End If
       
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 5, pe1.GetRight + 1,8, SagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2,"SURTEL KABLO SANAYÝÝ A.Þ.")

	SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH)
	CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	Set C00 = Doc.Select1("e3434", "CARI00", "KOD='" & STOK46E.CH_KODU & "'", "", -1)
	CHAdi = C00.AD
	Adres1 = C00.ADRES_1
	Adres2 = C00.ADRES_2
	Adres3 = C00.ADRES_3
	Tel = C00.TELEFONNO_1
	Faks = C00.FAXNO
	email = C00.EMAIL
	ilgili = STOK46E.SIPIALAN

	Gen1 = 34
	Gen2 = 76
	Aralik = 6
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+30, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Sipariþ No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6,Gen2,  ": " & trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen2,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH))
	sayfaNoUst = pe1.GetTop()

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tedarikçi Kodu")
	TedUst = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen2,": " & STOK46E.CH_KODU)
	Blok2 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Tedarikçi Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & CHAdi)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 1, 6,Gen1, "Sip. Veren")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, Gen2,": " & STOK46E.SIPIVEREN)

	Gen21 = 18
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(TedUst, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Telefon")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Tel)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Faks")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Faks)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "e-mail")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & email)

	if trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) = "" Then
		Odeme = "Peþin"
	Else
		if trim(STOK46E.FIYSEKLI) = "SP" Then
			Odeme = "Sipariþ tarihinden itibaren "
		Else
			Odeme = "Fatura tarihinden itibaren "
		End if
		Odeme = Odeme & trim(STOK46E.ODEMEGUNU) & " gün"
	End if
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, Blok2, 6, Gen21, "Ödeme")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & Odeme)
		
	Str1 = "Zamanýnda teslim etmek kaydýyla, aþaðýda dökümü yapýlan malzemelerin sipariþe alýnmasýný rica ederiz" & vbcrlf & _
			"Bozuk olan mallarýn iadesi yapýlacaktýr" & vbcrlf & "Lütfen faks ile teyit ediniz."

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 2, solMargin, -2, sagMargin - solMargin, Str1)
	
	BaslikBasla = pe1.GetBottom + 4
	satirBaslangic = BaslikBasla + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	Sol = SolMargin
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Redim xCizgi(0)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) = "" Then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			End if
		End if
		res = pe1.write(BaslikBasla, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi , Baslik)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		if i < uBound(Grid, 1) Then
			if Grid(i+1,2) <> "" Then
				xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)-1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	If siraNo >= sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If		
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = SolMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		Else
			If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
				Degisken = satirNo
			ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_FIYAT" Then
				Degisken = "LME+"
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End If
		End If	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 1.5, Degisken)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next					 
End Sub

'dýþ çerçeve

	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()

	KutuUst = pe1.GetTop()
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + 10, KutuSol, 0, KutuGen)
'	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, KutuSol + KutuGen/2, 10, 0)
	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Fikret ÇOBANOÐLU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, KutuSol, 5, KutuGen / 2, "GENEL MÜDÜR")

	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, pe1.GetRight, 5, KutuGen / 2, "Ani GÖÇER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, KutuGen / 2, "SATINALMA MÜDÜRÜ")

	res = pe1.Nopen()
'	res = pe1.Picture(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 10, 120, stdLogo, 114)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Century Gothic", 9)
	Adres = "Sefaköy Fevzi Çakmak Caddesi No.9 34630 Sefaköy - ÝSTANBUL - TURKEY"
	TelFaks = "Tel.: +90 212 624 32 46 (pbx)  Faks: +90 212 624 25 78 - 624 21 58"
	webMail = "www.surtel.com.tr  e-mail : info@surtel.com.tr"

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin, solMargin + 36, 0, 120)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 1, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, Adres)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, TelFaks)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 4.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft, webMail)
End Sub
